# Experiment ledger

One entry per plant2 experiment, in execution order. Every entry was drafted from the
adversarially verified extraction (`.agent/ledger/*.json`, workflow wf_87512f73-c2f, 2026-10-22)
and checked by an independent Opus critic against the primary sources it cites (contracts in
`docs/plant2/`, records in `bench/results/`, reviews in `review/`). Status vocabulary and the
rules for adding an entry are in `README.md`. Entries are append-only after the experiment
closes; corrections are appended as errata, never edited in place.

Contents: P2-E1, P2-E2, P2-E3, P2-E4, P2-E5, P2-R1, P2-E6, P2-R2, P2-R2b, P2-E7 (contracted), OFFLINE-SCREENS-20261022 (offline analyses).

## P2-E1: one-shot episodic storage by behavioural-timescale plasticity (BTSP), predeclared 2026-10-10

| field | value |
|---|---|
| status | complete |
| result label | FAIL (2026-10-10, commit 749caee, contract digest 1f2dcf7f19bf7073, all five seeds valid). C1 fails on every seed. C4 fails on its recall half, and C5 fails because C1 fails again after the 60 s. C2 and C3 pass on every seed. Addendum after independent review (2026-10-10): 'The verdict stands: FAIL, trustworthy.' STAGES.md:81 logs it as 'FAIL on C1 (completion 0.81-0.86 against 0.90) on 5/5 seeds; specificity and the unlearned-cue control pass. Diagnosis: no load-independent operating point (C1 0.61 at M = 250; everything ignites at M = 2,000).' Source disagreement: STAGES.md:81 names only C1 as the failing criterion and says 'everything ignites at M = 2,000', whereas docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:176-179 and plant2.jsonl:1-6 show that C1, C4 (recall half) and C5 all fail, and the M = 2,000 point has C1 0.975 with median ignition 113 of 4,000 cells (C2/C3 0.000; reported-only, n = 1, bench/results/plant2.jsonl:10). Decision per contract: J, f_q, rates, windows and bars stay as they are, this configuration is not re-run to pass, and the next Stage 1 experiment must carry a mechanism that holds each cell's operating point independent of load under its own contract. |

Test-hygiene fix: 1f6e057 'plant2 tests: outputs under ~/.cache/brain-sim/plant2/test-runs, never pytest tmp dirs (review P2-E1 F3)'. |

**Objective.** The experiment asks whether BTSP (Wu & Maass 2025, Nat Commun 16:342, Eq. 1; a dendritic plateau opens a window in which each active input synapse flips its binary weight with probability 0.5) stores M = 1,000 items shown once each so that a half cue re-evokes at least 80 % of the item's written assembly within 50 ms for at least 90 % of items. The conditions are Poisson spiking inputs, ongoing background, LIF integration, a 50 ms recall window and a synaptic strength fixed by a rule written before any run. Contract hypothesis (docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:30-32): 'A plasticity rule that writes in one exposure without depending on postsynaptic spike timing, with bounded binary weights and no homeostat acting on them, stores many items shown once each in a spiking network. Partial cues then re-evoke the written cells quickly and specifically.' Stage 1 gate it targets (docs/plant2/STAGES.md:45): half cue re-evokes >= 80 % of the written assembly within 50 ms for >= 90 % of items at a load of >= 0.25 items per memory cell; unlearned cues ignite < half an assembly; holds for the oldest items and after 60 s of ongoing activity. P2-E1 does not test the gate's further clauses: content completion, capacity growing about linearly with cell count, and the owner's 2026-10-10 additions (online recall while learning continues; item-specific recall on correlated inputs) (docs/plant2/STAGES.md:45). Testing is settled and offline only: learning is off and net.quiet() resets all dynamic state before each test, so P2-E1 says nothing about online recall while learning continues (docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:92, 300-302; plant2/experiments/p2_e1_btsp.py:96-102).

**Configuration.**

| name | value | source |
|---|---|---|
| `m_inp_poisson_sources` | 4000 (labelled proxy for the CA3/entorhinal input that carries an experience) | docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:54-57; plant2/experiments/p2_e1_btsp.py:20 (m=4000) |
| `n_mem_lif_cells` | 4000; tau_m 20.0 ms, v_rest -70.0 mV, v_th -50.0 mV (fixed), v_reset -65.0 mV, t_ref 2 ms, dt 1 ms, delta synapses with 1 ms delay | docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:58-60; plant2/experiments/p2_e1_btsp.py:19-23 (CONTRACT) |
| `a_active_inputs_per_item` | 100 of 4000 (f_p = 2.5 %) fire at r_on = 40.0 Hz; all others r_off = 0.5 Hz; items drawn uniformly without replacement per item, independent across items | docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:54-57; plant2/experiments/p2_e1_btsp.py:20 |
| `J_mV` | 1.12 mV per spike on a w = 1 synapse; w = 0 transmits nothing; every input-memory pair is a potential synapse (full connectivity); 'No other value of J is run.' | docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:61-71; plant2/experiments/p2_e1_btsp.py:21 (J=1.12) |
| `J_rule_inputs` | Target: predicted mean free-membrane depolarisation of a written cell under its half cue at M = 1,000 equals 1.4 x the 20 mV distance to threshold = 28 mV. Prediction used: P(Poisson(8) >= 3) = 0.98625 active-input eligibility; P(Poisson(0.1) >= 3) = 0.000155 background eligibility; 49.6 strong synapses written per plateau; 24.66 strong synapses on the half cue's 50 inputs; (M - 1) f_q = 5.0 other plateaus giving 247.8 strong synapses, 3.10 on the cue; input rate 40 x 27.76 + 0.5 x 269.8 = 1,245 Hz; J = 1.1245 rounded to 1.12 mV. Rule later found to double-count (see failures). | docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:64-71 |
| `M_items_learned` | 1000 (gated load = 0.25 items per memory cell), each shown once for t_item = 200 ms; episodes separated by quiet time that is not simulated | docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:73-76; plant2/experiments/p2_e1_btsp.py:21 |
| `f_q_plateau_probability_per_cell_per_episode` | 0.005 (Wu & Maass value from Grienberger & Magee 2022); plateaus from their own random stream, independent of content and cell activity; a plateau injects no current and makes no cell fire; A(x) = cells with a plateau for item x ('written assembly') | docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:78-84 |
| `eligibility_min_spikes_in_t_item` | 3 spikes in the 200 ms episode (labelled proxy) | docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:85-86; plant2/experiments/p2_e1_btsp.py:20 (elig_min=3) |
| `p_flip` | 0.5 (toggle 0->1 and 1->0, Wu & Maass Eq. 1); coin flips on their own random stream; update does not read memory-cell spikes | docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:87-90 |
| `test_protocol` | Learning off (no plateaus, no weight change). Cue set = 100 oldest items (1-100) + 100 drawn uniformly from items 101-1,000 with a fixed seed; novel set = 200 fresh random items never shown. Each cue shown 100 ms then 200 ms background. Half cue = fixed random 50 of the item's 100 inputs at 40 Hz (others background); full cue = all 100 (latency reference only). A cell 'responds' if it spikes at least once in [onset, onset + 50 ms). | docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:92-104; plant2/experiments/p2_e1_btsp.py:20 (n_old=100, n_rand=100, n_novel=200, t_cue=100, t_gap=200, window=50) |
| `per_item_measures` | recall(x) = \|responders to half cue intersect A(x)\| / \|A(x)\|; spurious(x) = \|responders to half cue not in A(x)\|; ignition(y) = \|responders to novel item y's half cue\| | docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:102-104 |
| `t_persist_ms` | 60000 (60 s of background-only activity, plateaus off, then C1-C3 re-measured on the same cues) | docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:118-121; plant2/experiments/p2_e1_btsp.py:22 |
| `BARS` | recall 0.80; item_frac 0.90; spurious_of_A 0.5; ignition_of_meanA 0.5; elig_valid 0.95; mean_A_lo 18.5; mean_A_hi 21.5 | plant2/experiments/p2_e1_btsp.py:24-25 (BARS); bench/results/plant2.jsonl:1 (bars) |
| `CONTRACT_dict_in_code` | m=4000, n=4000, a=100, r_on=40.0, r_off=0.5, t_item=200, elig_min=3, f_q=0.005, p_flip=0.5, J=1.12, M=1000, n_old=100, n_rand=100, n_novel=200, t_cue=100, t_gap=200, window=50, t_persist=60_000, tau_m=20.0, v_rest=-70.0, v_reset=-65.0, v_th=-50.0, t_ref=2; contract_digest 1f2dcf7f19bf7073 (identical in all 11 P2-E1 records) | plant2/experiments/p2_e1_btsp.py:19-23; bench/results/plant2.jsonl:1-11 |
| `kill_test_criteria_predeclared` | PASS only if every criterion holds on every seed 1-5. C1 completion: recall(x) >= 0.80 for >= 90 % of the 200 cued items. C2 specificity: spurious(x) < 0.5 \|A(x)\| for >= 90 % of cued items. C3: ignition(y) < 0.5 x mean \|A\| for >= 90 % of 200 novel items. C4: C1 and C2 also hold within the 100 oldest items alone (followed by >= 900 later writes). C5: after 60 s background-only, C1-C3 hold again on the same cues ('stated now as a property by construction, not as evidence of a consolidation mechanism'). | docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:106-121 |
| `validity_conditions_predeclared` | A failure makes the run invalid, not a result: mean eligible fraction of active inputs during learning >= 0.95; strong-synapse set bit-identical before and after every test phase; mean \|A\| over the 1,000 items within 20 +/- 1.5 | docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:123-125 |

A further Stage 1 experiment then needs a different mechanism and its own contract.' | docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:127-131 |

| `hub_definition_in_code` | cells with hits > 0.05 x (number of half cues + number of novel half cues) = more than 5 % of 400 half cues (200 learned + 200 novel), counted together | plant2/experiments/p2_e1_btsp.py:184-188 |

**Seeds.**

Exploration: None declared in the contract. Reported-not-gated capacity curve on seed 1 only (M = 250, 500, 1,000, 2,000, 4,000; J fixed at 1.12; n = 1 per point; bench/results/plant2.jsonl:7-11). Reviewer-run exploratory engine sweeps on seeds 1-2 (J sweeps, window widening, null windows, surrogates) were 'not recorded as results' and are not in plant2.jsonl (docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:291-299; review/plant2/P2-E1/findings-code.md:38; findings-direction.md:32).

Calibration: None. J = 1.12 mV was fixed by a rule written before any run ('No other value of J is run'); the contract declares no calibration or held-out seed (docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:64-71).

Gated: Seeds 1-5, each a separate network, item set, input-spike stream, plateau stream and coin stream; 'PASS only if every criterion holds on every seed.' All five seeds valid; all five FAIL (bench/results/plant2.jsonl:1-6).

Spent or reserved: Seeds 1-5 are spent for this configuration (gated, valid, FAIL; contract: 'this configuration is not re-run to pass', docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:250-253). The P2-E1 record names no reserved seeds. Reviewer proposals for later experiments named held-out seed 0 (direction lens, findings-direction.md:75) and seeds 6-10 (code lens, findings-code.md:63); these are proposals in review files, not reservations recorded in the contract or Result.

Notes: Reviewer exploratory sweeps used seeds 1-2, which are also gated seeds (no held-out seed existed in the P2-E1 design). STAGES.md and later P2-E2 rows are outside this record; P2-E2 is logged in STAGES.md:82 as using held-out calibration and 5 fresh gated seeds.

**Failures and errata.**

| what | kind | detail | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| C1 completion fails on all five gated seeds (0.810-0.860 vs bar 0.90) at M = 1,000, J = 1.12 | failed_hypothesis | Written cells fire but too late: median recall 0.92-0.94 at 50 ms, 1.00 at 100 ms, 0.42-0.48 at 25 ms (original diagnosis). Pooled written-cell recall rises with total plateau count k (0.73 at k = 1 to 0.98 at k >= 12). Corrected figures: cell-level miss 10.2-11.9 %, failing items have 3-14 late cells. Reviewer exploratory sweeps: no single fixed J passes C1-C4 at M = 1,000 or at both M = 250 and M = 1,000; widening the window to 75 ms trades C1 0.99 for C2 0.72. | docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:176-179, 213-219, 283-299; bench/results/plant2.jsonl:1-6 |
| C4 recall half fails on all five seeds (0.820, 0.830, 0.890, 0.820, 0.880 vs bar 0.90) | failed_hypothesis | Oldest 100 items fail the same completion bar as the rest; C4 spurious half passes (0.920-0.950). Medians for old items (0.907-0.946) are within 0.03 of the rest (0.915-0.929). | docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:177-178, 184-188, 209; bench/results/plant2.jsonl:1-5 |
| C5 fails on all five seeds because C1 fails again after 60 s (0.825-0.880) | failed_hypothesis | Consequence of the C1 failure, not of any drift. The weights cannot change without a plateau and present() resets all dynamic state, so C5 equals C1 re-measured with a new input-noise draw (draw-to-draw noise about 0.02). | docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:177-179, 300-305; review/plant2/P2-E1/findings-code.md:23-27 |
| C5 and the never-trained twin cannot discriminate (design could not answer the persistence and null-response questions) | inadequate_experiment | present() calls net.quiet() which zeroes v, the delay ring and refractory state before each test; the 60,000-tick loop (about half the wall time) changes nothing measured. The twin has all-zero weights so 0 responders is guaranteed. Real nulls (background-only windows, wrong-cue) were never run in the experiment; reviewer ran them post hoc (0 responders in 200 windows; wrong-cue recall 0.006). | docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:300-307; review/plant2/P2-E1/findings-code.md:23-33; findings-methodology.md:17-21, 59-63 |
| Capacity curve and 'no operating point' claim rest on n = 1 seed at one J; the single gated load cannot distinguish a missing operating point from a thin margin | inadequate_experiment | Capacity points are nested prefixes of seed 1 at fixed J = 1.12 (n = 1 each). P2-E1 gated a single load (M = 1,000); direction lens F4: it 'cannot tell a missing operating point from a thin margin'. Support for the claim is reviewer exploratory sweeps on seeds 1-2 not recorded as results. | docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:291-299; review/plant2/P2-E1/findings-code.md:35-39; findings-direction.md:29-33 |
| ERRATUM: J rule double-counted 3.1 cue synapses; the 1.4x target was really 1.24x | interpretive_error_corrected | BTSP toggling leaves each of a written cell's own item inputs at P(strong) = 0.5 whatever later plateaus do, so later items' strong synapses on the cue lines are the same synapses, not extra ones. Measured on seed 1: 24.69 cue synapses per written cell (sd 3.55), flat in k (24.5-24.8), not the predicted 27.76. Mean half-cue drive about 24.9 mV, 1.24x the distance to threshold, not 1.4x; the rule computed correctly gives J of about 1.26 mV. Verdict unaffected because J = 1.12 was fixed explicitly, but the Result line 'C1 the criterion most at risk: right' is 'an artefact of the error'. Reviewer sweep at J = 1.25 (exploratory, seed 1): C1 0.99, C2 0.445, C3 0.57, so at the intended margin the run fails on specificity instead. | docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:272-282; review/plant2/P2-E1/findings-methodology.md:11-15; findings-code.md:11-15 |
| ERRATUM: 'About 1 cell in 13 crosses threshold after the 50 ms window' and 'failing items are mostly marginal (recall 0.70-0.79, one or two of about 20 cells late)' | interpretive_error_corrected | Cell-level miss rate is 10.2-11.9 % (about 1 in 9); '1 in 13' was the median item recall. Failing items have 3-14 late cells (median 5-6) and 34-45 % of failing items have recall below 0.70. k counts all of the cell's plateaus, this item's included (the Result said plateaus 'for other items'). | docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:213-219, 283-290; review/plant2/P2-E1/findings-methodology.md:35-39; review/ledger.jsonl row 5 |
| ERRATUM: 'nothing erased by 60 s of ongoing activity' listed among what the run shows | interpretive_error_corrected | Restated as a design property, not evidence (C5 vacuous). Reviewer nulls with teeth on the seed-1 store: 0 responders in 200 background-only windows; another item's half cue recalls 0.006 of A(x). | docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:245, 300-307; review/plant2/P2-E1/findings-methodology.md:17-21 |
| ERRATUM: overstated capability bullets ('the right cells, with about 2 spurious per cue'; 'no response to unlearned cues'; 'full cues recalled at 1.00 within 10 ms') | interpretive_error_corrected | Restated as measured: unlearned cues median ignition 2, maximum 15-21 per seed (about one assembly), C3 0.945-0.98; full cues median first-spike latency 10 ms with recall 1.00 at 50 ms; spurious median 2 per cue, with C2 at 0.905 and 0.900 on seed 5. | docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:240-246, 308-312; review/plant2/P2-E1/findings-methodology.md:41-45 |
| ERRATUM: 'no operating point' presented as shown by the run | interpretive_error_corrected | Addendum item 3: it 'rests on reviewer sweeps, not on the run'; the capacity curve is one seed (n = 1 per point). Methodology F4: the 'fire but too late' diagnosis is incomplete; reword as half-cue drive spread (coefficient of variation 14 %, from the p = 0.5 flip and the 50 % mask) plus load-dependent background leaving no separable window at M = 1,000. | docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:291-299; review/plant2/P2-E1/findings-methodology.md:29-33 |

A mechanism that tracks mean potential removes only the background part. | docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:296-298; review/plant2/P2-E1/findings-direction.md:11-15 |

**Reproducibility.**

Commands:

```
python3 -m plant2.experiments.p2_e1_btsp   # kill test, seeds 1-5 (docstring, plant2/experiments/p2_e1_btsp.py:3)
python3 -m plant2.experiments.p2_e1_btsp --capacity   # reported capacity curve, seed 1 (docstring, plant2/experiments/p2_e1_btsp.py:4)
python3 -m plant2.experiments.p2_e1_btsp --seeds <int ...>   # argparse option, plant2/experiments/p2_e1_btsp.py:324
python3 -m pytest -q plant2/tests -m "not slow"   # fast plant2 tests per AGENTS.md; the P2-E1 kill-test wrapper in plant2/tests/test_p2_e1.py is marked slow (docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:169-170). Not run by this extraction.
```

Artifacts:

- bench/results/plant2.jsonl lines 1-5 (kind kill_test_seed, seeds 1-5), line 6 (kind kill_test_verdict), lines 7-11 (kind capacity_point, M = 250, 500, 1000, 2000, 4000)
- docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md (contract lines 1-172 frozen; Result 174-258; addendum 260-323)
- review/plant2/P2-E1/findings-code.md, findings-direction.md, findings-methodology.md (verbatim reviews)
- review/ledger.jsonl rows 1-29 (review P2-E1: methodology rows 1-10, code rows 11-19, direction rows 20-29) (supplementary, outside the named source list; read for dispositions)
- plant2/experiments/p2_e1_btsp.py, plant2/btsp.py, plant2/engine.py, plant2/record.py, plant2/tests/test_p2_e1.py
- docs/plant2/AUDIT-2026-10-10.md (supplementary, lines 1-10 read only for the provenance wording)
- DECISIONS.md lines 248-290 (supplementary, outside the assigned 214-247 range; P2-E2 gate entry citing P2-E1's capacity curve and review)
- Intended per-item arrays and logs (not present on this machine): ~/.cache/brain-sim/plant2/p2_e1/seed{1-5}.npz (A_size, recall50, spurious, ignition, recall50_after, plateaus_per_cell, strong_per_cell), kill_test.log, capacity.log (plant2/experiments/p2_e1_btsp.py:274-277, 326)

Caches present or missing: The per-item cache directory ~/.cache/brain-sim/plant2/p2_e1/ named in the Result (docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:179-180) does NOT exist on this machine: ~/.cache/brain-sim/plant2/ is absent (BRAINSIM_CACHE unset, so record.cache_dir would use that default). ~/.cache/brain-sim/plant2-research-20261022/ exists but holds only tests/ outputs (pytest/junit logs and test-runs dirs), no P2-E1 seed npz or logs. /tmp/pytest-of-* does not exist here. So the per-item arrays (npz) and kill_test.log/capacity.log cannot be read here; every number in this record comes from docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md, the review files and bench/results/plant2.jsonl (which carries per-seed criteria, validity, report and learning summaries). Reviewers recomputed from the npz in their own environment.

Caveats: Reproduction is not a new accepted attempt. The contract forbids re-running this configuration to pass ('this configuration is not re-run to pass'); executing the driver would append further P2-E1 kill_test_seed/kill_test_verdict/capacity_point records to the append-only bench/results/plant2.jsonl through record.append. The original records omit numpy and python versions (code F7); record.runtime() was added later and its docstring states 'digests depend on numpy's SIMD kernels (AUDIT 2026-10-10)'. The code reviewer's rerun reproduced seed-1 learning and C1 0.840 bit-exact and store size 930,107 under numpy 2.5.3. Capacity points are nested prefixes of seed 1, so the M = 1,000 point reproduces the kill-test seed-1 numbers exactly. All reviewer sweeps, nulls, surrogates and proposed-experiment simulations (J sweeps, J = 1.25, window widening, background-only windows, wrong-cue null, item-level surrogate, accommodation and oracle-threshold stand-ins) were not recorded in plant2.jsonl and have no committed artifact in the named sources.

**Conclusions.**

- P2-E1 FAILS the Stage 1 kill test: C1 fails on 5/5 valid seeds (0.810-0.860 vs 0.90); C4 recall half fails (0.820-0.890); C5 fails (C1 after 60 s 0.825-0.880); C2 (0.905-0.950) and C3 (0.945-0.980) pass on 5/5 seeds. The verdict was independently reviewed and 'stands: FAIL, trustworthy'. (docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:176-179, 264)
- Diagnosis in the Result: written cells fire but too late at M = 1,000 (median recall 0.92-0.94 at 50 ms, 1.00 at 100 ms, 0.42-0.48 at 25 ms); each cell's excitability is set by how much the network has stored, not by the cue; 'It is the absence of an operating point' rather than a margin problem at one load (capacity curve: C1 0.610 at M = 250, 0.840 at M = 1,000; C2/C3 0.000 at M = 2,000). Addendum item 3: this rests on reviewer exploratory sweeps and an n = 1 capacity curve, not on the run. (docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:213-238, 291-299)
- The contract's J rule double-counted 3.1 cue synapses; actual mean half-cue drive was about 24.9 mV (1.24x), not 28 mV (1.4x); the rule computed correctly gives J of about 1.26 mV; at that margin a reviewer's exploratory seed-1 sweep (J = 1.25) fails on specificity (C2 0.445, C3 0.57) rather than completion (C1 0.99). (docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:272-282)
- What the run shows, as restated after review: BTSP writes one-shot traces in a spiking network with median 2 spurious per cue (C2 0.905-0.950), median novel-cue ignition 2 (maximum 15-21 per seed; C3 0.945-0.98), old-item median recall within 0.03 of the rest, full-cue median first-spike latency 10 ms with recall 1.00 at 50 ms; the 60 s persistence result is a design property, not evidence. 'None of that is the Stage 1 gate.' (docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:240-248, 308-312)
- Decision per contract: J = 1.12, f_q, rates, windows and bars unchanged; this configuration is not re-run to pass; a missing mechanism that holds each cell's operating point independent of load is indicated; the next Stage 1 experiment carries such a mechanism under its own contract. (docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:250-253)
- Stage 1 tests whether a code assigned by random plateaus can be read back from a partial cue; choosing its own representations is Stage 3's question; a Stage 1 pass is not evidence of thinking power. Linear capacity growth with cell count needs a signal-to-noise mechanism of its own and no operating-point mechanism gets credit for it. (docs/plant2/STAGES.md:54-60; docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:320-323)
- Review outcome: three fresh reviewers (methodology, code, direction) judged the FAIL trustworthy; contract preceded code, code preceded runs; implementation verified by dense replays (0 mismatches); proposed next mechanisms: slow per-cell threshold accommodation (direction, methodology lenses; accepted in part, became P2-E2) and best-of-2 load-balanced plateau allocation (code lens; deferred). (docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:260-268; review/ledger.jsonl rows 10, 19; DECISIONS.md:255-260, outside the assigned range)

**Open questions.**

- What mechanism holds each memory cell's operating point independent of load? The Result says the diagnosis 'points to a missing mechanism that holds each cell's operating point independent of load', and the next Stage 1 experiment 'carries such a mechanism under its own contract' (docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:250-253).
- Does the failure persist across seeds beyond the reviewers' 1-2 and across more than one J? Only exploratory reviewer sweeps (not recorded as results) show no J passes C1-C4 at M = 1,000 or at both M = 250 and 1,000 (docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:291-299).
- How much of the load drift is background vs other items' strong synapses on the cue's own lines (reviewers: about equal, about 45 % cue-line; a mean-potential-tracking mechanism removes only the background part, and the cue-line part sets the C2/C3 capacity edge, which direction F1 asks to be predicted)? The record does not reconcile addendum item 1 (later items' synapses on cue lines are the same synapses, P(strong) = 0.5, flat in k) with item 3 / direction F1 (cue-line synapses drive about half the load effect); that is an observation by this extractor, not a statement in the record (review/plant2/P2-E1/findings-direction.md:11-15; docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:272-298).
- Can a Stage 1 load-independence gate be tested directly (one predeclared J and tau passing C1-C5 at M = 250, 500 and 1,000 on all 5 seeds)? Direction F4 recommends it; P2-E2 later gated C1-C4 at M = 250, 500, 1,000 (review/ledger.jsonl row 23).
- Can the C5 persistence question be asked with teeth? Only when the mechanism has state that can drift (adaptation, inhibitory plasticity), starting phase 2 from the post-60 s state and with quiet() never resetting adaptive variables (review/plant2/P2-E1/findings-code.md:27; findings-methodology.md:21).
- Linear capacity growth with cell count needs 'a signal-to-noise mechanism of its own: sparser codes over more inputs, recurrent completion with a stabiliser, or matched inhibition'; no operating-point mechanism gets credit for it (docs/plant2/STAGES.md:58-60; findings-direction.md:53-57).
- How does a continuous stream with a 10 s window spanning neighbouring items change storage? Deferred to Stage 2 contracts (review/ledger.jsonl row 8).

**Experimenter supply remaining.**

- The input code: the item patterns (100 of 4,000 inputs at 40 Hz, others 0.5 Hz, drawn uniformly without replacement) as a labelled proxy for the CA3/entorhinal input that carries an experience (docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:54-57).
- The plateau set A(x): which memory cells get a plateau for each item (f_q = 0.005 per cell per episode), drawn from an independent random stream; labelled proxy for entorhinal-layer-III instructive input; 'a random code assigned by content-independent plateaus' (docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:78-84, 320-323).
- The eligibility rule and window: input eligible if it spiked >= 3 times in the 200 ms episode; labelled proxy for the seconds-long eligibility trace (docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:85-86).
- Episode segmentation: one item per 200 ms episode with state reset between items and quiet time not simulated; 'segmentation comes from the protocol' (docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:75-76; findings-methodology.md:53-57).
- The write rule constants: p_flip = 0.5, binary weights, bounded strength J = 1.12 mV fixed by a rule (a rule later shown to double-count cue synapses), fixed threshold -50 mV, full connectivity (docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:61-71, 87-90).
- The recall protocol and criteria: fixed random half-cue masks, 50 ms response window, cue set (100 oldest + 100 random), novel set, all bars (0.80, 0.90, 0.5, 0.5), the 60 s persistence test (docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:92-125).
- The operating point: J 'was placed by its rule at the only load where the two effects nearly balance' (M = 1,000); the experimenter, not the system, set each cell's operating point (docs/plant2/P2-E1-btsp.md:235-238).
- Stated in STAGES.md:71 as the owner's standing test, 'Every experiment is judged by whether it reduces the cognition the experimenter supplies'; the P2-E1 record contains no explicit assessment of this for P2-E1 itself beyond the labelled proxies above.

**Capabilities and hypotheses.** Capabilities claimed by this record are listed in RESEARCH-MAP.md; hypotheses in HYPOTHESES.md.


## P2-E2: a load-independent operating point by slow threshold accommodation (predeclared 2026-10-10)

| field | value |
|---|---|
| status | complete. The gated verdict is PASS, valid and not void, and the owner's ruling lets the PASS stand (DECISIONS.md:312-318). README's definition of 'complete' is not met to the letter, for two reasons. First, paired seed 5's record is INVALID: its convergence was 0.207 mV against a 0.2 mV bar before the reported-only M = 2,000 phase. Second, two reported items that the contract promised were not produced: the random store at every load, and a separate 100-pair wrong-cue null. Neither affects a gated measurement or a gated validity check. See Failures and errata. |
| result label | PASS (2026-10-10). The run is valid and not void. J* = 1.525 mV. All five gated seeds (6-10) pass every gated criterion at M = 250, 500 and 1,000, and C5 holds; on every gated seed both void controls fail as the contract requires (P2-E2-accommodation.md:223-225). Addendum after independent review (2026-10-10): "The verdict stands: PASS, trustworthy." (line 338-342). Decision per contract: "Accommodation is plant2's operating-point mechanism", holding pending the owner's gate ruling (line 331-334), which then came: DECISIONS.md:312-318 "P2-E2 is accepted as a distinct mechanism, and K0.14 is not reopened ... The P2-E2 PASS stands with every limitation in its addendum." Seed 5 (paired, reported) record: INVALID. |
| stage | Stage 1 (one-shot episodic memory), second experiment of the plant2 line. | mechanism under test | Slow threshold accommodation (labelled proxy). Per memory cell i, every tick: vbar_i <- vbar_i + (v_i - vbar_i)/tau_acc; theta_i = v_th + (vbar_i - v_rest); spike if v_i >= theta_i and not refractory. vbar is updated after the membrane update and before the spike test. tau_acc = 10 s (10,000 ticks); v_th = -50 mV; v_rest = -70 mV (P2-E1 values). net.quiet() never resets vbar; no test phase calls quiet(). Everything else is P2-E1 unchanged (BTSP with random plateau gating f_q 0.005, binary inputs-to-memory synapses, items, half-cue protocol, 50 ms window, bars, random streams). Source: docs/plant2/P2-E2-accommodation.md:35-61. |

**Objective.** Remove the load-dependent background offset of each memory cell's spike threshold so that one J, calibrated on a held-out seed, gives half-cue completion and specificity at M = 250, 500 and 1,000 stored items, where P2-E1 (FAIL on C1, 0.81-0.86 against 0.90 on 5/5 seeds; C1 0.61 at M = 250; C2 = C3 = 0.00 at M = 2,000) found no fixed J working across loads. Contract hypothesis (docs/plant2/P2-E2-accommodation.md:30-33): "Each memory cell's spike threshold slowly tracks its own mean membrane potential. That removes the steady, load-dependent background offset cell by cell. With one J, calibrated in advance on a held-out seed, half-cue completion and specificity then hold at 250, 500 and 1,000 stored items, where no fixed threshold at that J does."

| convergence window and bar | mean over cells of \|vbar change\| over last 10 s of settling <= 0.2 mV (CONVERGE_WINDOW_MS 10_000, CONVERGE_BAR_MV 0.2) | P2-E2-accommodation.md:136-137; p2_e2_accommodation.py:24-25 |

**Seeds.**

**Exploration.** Reviewer exploratory runs behind the contract (P2-E1 review, F7 accommodation at 1-3 s habituates): not recorded as results. Post-result reviewer exploratory runs (all labelled exploratory, unrecorded): seed 0 (methodology: M = 1,000 rest-time test, offsets, fixed-J check M = 250 needs fixed J >= 1.25, none in 1.10-1.35 passes at M = 1,000); seed 6 (code: 100-repeat habituation, 10 items, 1 seed, M = 1,000); seed 1 (re-learned with P2-E1 code); seeds 42-43 (direction: two-step Hebbian feedback content-completion exploration); addendum item 2 names 'reviewers' exploratory sweeps (seeds 0-2)' as the basis of 'no single fixed J passes 250 and 1,000 together'.

**Calibration.** Seed 0, held-out, 'never gated'; 33 calibration_point records (11 J x 3 loads) plus 1 calibration_verdict (record counts via grep of bench/results/plant2.jsonl); code commit 6076a22; passing run 1.45-1.60; J* = 1.525; accommodation converged at every point (mean |vbar change| 0.03-0.09 mV against 0.2 mV bar).

**Gated.** Seeds 6, 7, 8, 9, 10, 'all fresh'; all five pass every gated criterion at M = 250, 500, 1,000 plus C5; both void controls fail as required on every gated seed. Single J* = 1.525.

**Spent or reserved.** Spent: 0 (calibration; also reused in later reviewer exploration), 1-5 (reported, paired; P2-E1's gated seeds), 6-10 (gated). Seeds 11-15 were proposed as fresh by all three reviewers for the next experiment (findings-code/direction/methodology 'Proposed next experiment'); P2-E3's own gated seeds were 11-15 per docs/plant2/P2-E3-content-completion.md:131, 320, 335 and plant2/experiments/p2_e3_completion.py:34 (review/ledger.jsonl:98 names P2-E3 gated seed 11; outside the P2-E2 contract). Contract/DECISIONS standing discipline: 'Gated seeds are fresh' (DECISIONS.md:345), so seeds 6-10 are spent for gating.

**Notes.** Seeds 1-5 are P2-E1's seeds; their stores equal P2-E1's; run at J* as a paired comparison, 'reported, not gated'. Seeds 1-4 pass every gated criterion; seed 5's record is marked INVALID (accommodation converged to 0.207 mV before the M = 2,000 test phase, bar 0.2 mV; that phase reported only; seed 5's gated loads converged at 0.030-0.085 mV and passed every criterion); seed 5's kill_test_seed row carries passed false, valid false (bench/results/plant2.jsonl). Reported-load phases M = 1,500 and 2,000 are reported, not gated.

**Controls.**

| name | purpose | outcome | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| V-A fixed-threshold arm (void condition) | Same stores, same J*, same input-spike streams, no accommodation; must fail C2 or C3 at M = 1,000 on every gated seed or the run is VOID (the mechanism must matter). | Fails as required on every gated seed: C1 1.000, C2 0.000-0.015, C3 0.000. Prediction was C2 <= 0.10 and C1 >= 0.95 at every load. Addendum: the fixed arm at J* passes M = 250 and 500 on every gated seed (C1 >= 0.995, C2 >= 0.905, C3 >= 0.92) and fails only at M = 1,000 (C2 <= 0.015); reviewers: V-A at the mechanism's own J was near-certain to fail, and it shows J* is too high for a fixed threshold, not that no fixed J works. | P2-E2-accommodation.md:122-128, 152-153, 246-248, 367-379; findings-methodology.md F2; findings-code.md F5 |
| V-B matched-density random store (void condition) | Each memory cell keeps its number of strong synapses but draws presynaptic inputs uniformly from its own stream; with accommodation and J* it must fail C1 at M = 1,000 on every gated seed (the memory must matter). | Fails as required on every gated seed: C1 0.000, median recall 0.0 (prediction median recall <= 0.10). Addendum: fails by construction because it removes the cue-line-to-assembly synapses (presynaptic out-degree sd 14.7 vs 46 real); a control that keeps cue-line synapses and shuffles only the cross-talk is the stronger null. Contract promised the random arm at every load; code ran it at M = 1,000 only. | P2-E2-accommodation.md:126-128, 249-250, 376-377, 425-426; findings-code.md F5 |

**Failures and errata**

| Contract's closed-gate table misstated K0.14 ('gain-like', 'no setpoint'); DECISIONS.md:262-267 repeated 'no setpoint and no spike count' | interpretive_error_corrected | Addendum item 1 and DECISIONS.md:275-286 correction: K0.14's theta_h was also additive ('added to theta in the spike test', SPEC around line 2600); P2-E2 does hold a setpoint (keeps theta - vbar at 20 mV). Accurate statement: both are slow, per-cell, additive intrinsic-threshold homeostats, differing in controlled variable (mean membrane potential, continuous and unbounded, vs firing rate per 1 s sweep, bounded), role (beside BTSP vs replacing scaling next to STDP) and network. Offset correlates 0.994-0.998 with stored strong-synapse count: 'in effect a per-cell load normaliser'. Two reviewers: materially different from K0.14 as run, squarely in the gated family. **Reproducibility.**

Commands:

```
python3 -m plant2.experiments.p2_e2_accommodation calibrate   # seed 0, J grid -> J* (driver docstring, p2_e2_accommodation.py:7)
python3 -m plant2.experiments.p2_e2_accommodation run --J 1.6 --seeds 6 7 8 9 10 --gated   # docstring text; J* was frozen at 1.525, so the docstring J is stale; --verdict flag appends the verdict row (p2_e2_accommodation.py:8,298)
python3 -m plant2.experiments.p2_e2_accommodation run --J 1.6 --seeds 1 2 3 4 5   # paired seeds, docstring text (same stale J)
git diff 19776f9 545d507 -- plant2   # code identity check named in the Result; run here with --stat: empty
```

The exact command lines used for the gated run and the paired run are not recorded in the named sources; logs that would show them are missing (see caches).

Caveats: Records written during the run name later HEADs (6e1fc99 ... 545d507) and carry dirty = true (results file uncommitted); code identity rests on the empty git diff 19776f9 545d507 -- plant2. The original verdict row lacks contract_digest and runtime; the Result's digest is P2-E1's. Reviewer evidence for rest-time, habituation, offsets, fixed-J sweeps and the capacity surrogate is exploratory and unrecorded. Reproduction of seeds 6-10 would be a reproduction only: DECISIONS.md:345-346 standing discipline says 'Gated seeds are fresh' and 'Passing thresholds are never revised'; no new accepted gated attempt is possible on spent seeds (my reading of that discipline, not an explicit P2-E2 rule). The reviewer's independent dense LIF re-implementation reproduced seed 6 exactly at M = 250, 500, 1,000.

**Conclusions.**

- PASS, valid, not void: single J* = 1.525 mV passes C1-C4 at M = 250, 500, 1,000 and C5 on 5/5 fresh gated seeds (6-10); both void controls fail (P2-E2-accommodation.md:223-225).
- Result sentence of record: in a spiking network one exposure per item stores up to 1,000 items in 4,000 memory cells (0.25 items per cell; load window from 0.0625 per addendum); half cue re-evokes >= 80 % of an item's written cells within 50 ms for >= 94.5 % of items (>= 91.5 % after 60 s) on gated seeds; unlearned cues ignite a median of 0-1 cells (max 14 of 4,000) (lines 316-320, 412-417).
- Accommodation extends the fixed-J window from <= 0.125 to 0.25 items per cell; a fixed threshold at J* passes M = 250 and 500 and fails only at M = 1,000 (lines 368-372).
- The offset tracks each cell's stored load (correlation 0.998; 0.994-0.998 in addendum): 'in effect a per-cell load normaliser, reached through the membrane potential' (lines 362-363).
- Reported, not gated: the limit moved to specificity: C2/C3 fall at M = 1,500 (C2 0.715-0.775) and 2,000 (C2 0.30-0.37); other memories' strong synapses on the cue's own lines end the window between 0.25 and 0.375 items per cell (lines 292-301).
- The operating point needs 5-50 s of rest after learning; C1 0.875 with none (exploratory); online protocol untested (lines 380-391).
- Owner rulings 2026-10-10: P2-E2 accepted as a distinct mechanism, K0.14 stays closed, family membership recorded; capacity primary experiment scales input and memory populations together with constant absolute active inputs and assembly size, memory-only scaling as control; P2-E3 may use fixed activity-proportional inhibition with matched no-inhibition and shuffled-feedback comparisons and frozen parameters (DECISIONS.md:308-331; STAGES.md:87-101).
- Stage 1 storage-and-recall part met; content-completion and capacity parts of the Stage 1 gate remained open at P2-E2 close (P2-E2-accommodation.md:328-329). Stage 1 as later amended by the owner also requires online recall with learning continuing and item-specific recall on correlated inputs (STAGES.md:45, 96-101).
- Order of work set by owner: P2-E3 (content completion) first (DECISIONS.md:333-335); a separate predeclared online-memory experiment must pass before Stage 1 counts as complete (DECISIONS.md:336-339); after P2-E3, an evaluation against the north star (DECISIONS.md:340-341); STAGES.md:96-99 gives the sequence P2-E3, then the evaluation, then online memory and capacity; P2-E2's contract and result are not changed retroactively (DECISIONS.md:339).

**Open questions.**

- Does the operating point hold online, with background simulated between episodes and no settle? (addendum item 3; contract untested. STAGES.md:84 later reports P2-E4 ONLINE INDEX FAIL, outside this record.)
- How far does habituation under a sustained or repeated cue go on gated seeds? Review proposed a 100-repeat arm as a reported measure in the next contract (review/ledger.jsonl:39; findings-code.md F1).
- What does 'capacity grows with cell count' scale, and does growth survive the full LIF simulation? Owner ruling: scale input and memory populations together, memory-only scaling as control (DECISIONS.md:319-325).
- Does the input content regenerate from a half cue (content completion)? Next Stage 1 experiment per Result decision (lines 331-334); STAGES.md:83 records P2-E3 as the later experiment.
- What is a persistence criterion that can fail for a real reason? Needs ongoing learning or a load step inside the interval (addendum item 5).
- A recorded fixed-J sweep on the held-out seed (M = 250/500/1,000) and a control that keeps cue-line synapses and shuffles only cross-talk were recommended and not run (findings-code.md F5; findings-methodology.md F2).
- Offsets at cue onset (not only after cue blocks) to be reported in later contracts; convergence to be gated on signed drift (review/ledger.jsonl:33,36).
- Is paired seed 5's 0.207 mV convergence at M = 2,000 noise or a real violation? (addendum item 9; unresolved).
- Does an optimistic window for Stage 2 exist given BTSP's real window and 200 ms items (direction F6)?
- Per-seed store digests in P2-E1-type records so later experiments can cross-check committed artifacts rather than re-running the same code (findings-methodology.md F8).

**Experimenter supply remaining.**

- Named by record: the code is assigned by random plateaus ('the plateaus are random'), so the system did not choose its own code; the assembly A(x) read back is the experimenter-assigned set of written cells (P2-E2-accommodation.md:323; STAGES.md:55-56: 'a code assigned by random plateaus').
- Named by record: input content is not regenerated ('not tested yet'); the half cue recovers the assigned assembly, not the missing input features (P2-E2-accommodation.md:324; DECISIONS.md:334-335).
- Named by record: the rest schedule, i.e. a 50 s settle before each test phase, because learning simulates only the 200 ms episodes and vbar is not at equilibrium (addendum item 3).
- Named by record: J, calibrated by the experimenter's predeclared midpoint rule on a held-out seed (J* = 1.525), rather than set by the network.
- Named by record: accommodation holds a setpoint, theta - vbar = 20 mV (v_th - v_rest), and tau_acc = 10 s, both chosen by the experimenter (addendum item 1; contract lines 38-49).
- Contract-fixed elements the record does not label as supply: item generation (a = 100 of 4,000 lines), half-cue masks and cue sets, cue timing (100 ms cue, 200 ms gap, 50 ms window), BTSP write parameters (f_q 0.005, p_flip 0.5, elig_min 3, t_item 200), the test order and no-quiet() protocol.

**Capabilities and hypotheses.** Capabilities claimed by this record are listed in RESEARCH-MAP.md; hypotheses in HYPOTHESES.md.

**Objective.** Primary (docs/plant2/P2-E3-content-completion.md:26-29): 'A memory-to-reconstruction feedback path, written one-shot from the memory cells' own spikes while the experience is still present, lets a half cue regenerate the missing half of the item's input features in a reconstruction layer. This holds at 250, 500 and 1,000 stored items, with P2-E2's forward store unchanged.' Secondary (read from the matched comparison, not gated, :31-32): 'The fixed activity-proportional inhibition is what makes the regeneration specific.'

**Configuration**

| name | value | source |
|---|---|---|
| rec layer | m = 4,000 LIF cells, one per input line; tau_m 20 ms, rest -70 mV, fixed threshold -50 mV, reset -65 mV, refractory 2 ms, plant's exact-decay update; no accommodation, no floor on v; receives no sensory spikes; not simulated during learning; starts at rest at the start of each test phase after the settle, never reset within a phase | docs/plant2/P2-E3-content-completion.md:46-52 |
| unchanged from P2-E2 | inputs, memory cells, BTSP forward store and its random streams; J* = 1.525 mV and threshold accommodation (tau 10 s); cue sets, 50 s settle, memory criteria; p2_e1_btsp.py and p2_e2_accommodation.py not edited | docs/plant2/P2-E3-content-completion.md:36-40 |
| changed vs P2-E2 | learning episodes last 250 ms instead of 200 ms (200 ms encoding + 50 ms continuation, no quiet() between); forward store bit-identical to P2-E1's; memory-layer activity differs slightly because the continuation moves vbar during learning (D5 measures it) | docs/plant2/P2-E3-content-completion.md:42-44, 57-62 |
| continuation input stream | stream(seed, 9, k) for item k (CONT = 9; P2-E1 uses ids 0-6, P2-E2 uses 8; SHUFFLED = 10; PERMUTED = 11) | docs/plant2/P2-E3-content-completion.md:58-60; plant2/experiments/p2_e3_completion.py:35 |
| write sets | R(x) = memory cells with >= 1 spike in the continuation; E(x) = lines with >= 3 spikes in the encoding, taken from encoding spike counts never from item indices; w_fb(i -> j) = 1 for all i in R(x), j in E(x); about 0.6 background lines per item reach eligibility and score as intrusions | docs/plant2/P2-E3-content-completion.md:64-76 |
| feedback path | binary, strength J_fb, delay 1 ms | docs/plant2/P2-E3-content-completion.md:54 |
| inhibition | each memory spike at tick t delivers -g x J_fb to every rec cell at tick t + 2 (excitation arrives t + 1); g = 0.3 fixed; g first found with same-tick inhibition, so the Willshaw-threshold description is approximate | docs/plant2/P2-E3-content-completion.md:77-87 |
| readout implementation | all arms replayed from the test phase's memory spike raster; shared feedback matrix turns each tick's memory spikes into integer counts per line; arm adds J x count; inhibition one scalar per tick per arm; same code for calibration and gated runs; unit test spike-for-spike against plant2.engine LIF + Projection | docs/plant2/P2-E3-content-completion.md:89-94 |
| test phase | learning always continues from the untested state; every test phase on a deep copy discarded afterwards; 50 s settle; 200 half cues of learned items (items 1-100 plus 100 drawn from the rest, fixed masks); 200 novel half cues; 200 full cues; cue 100 ms, gap 200 ms, 200 ms lead-in per block. Not run: P2-E2's 60 s phase, V-A, V-B, repeated-cue arms | docs/plant2/P2-E3-content-completion.md:96-113 |
| scoring window | onset = first tick with cue rates; a rec cell regenerates its line if it spikes in ticks k = 0..74 after onset; earliest causal spike k = 2 | docs/plant2/P2-E3-content-completion.md:115-118 |
| item sets | missing half = the item's 50 lines not in the half cue (non-eligible lines count as misses); visible half = 50 cue lines; intrusions = regenerated lines outside the item's 100 | docs/plant2/P2-E3-content-completion.md:120-124 |
| joint criterion (one item) | >= 80 % of the missing half regenerated (>= 40 of 50) and fewer than 10 intrusions | docs/plant2/P2-E3-content-completion.md:126-127; plant2/experiments/p2_e3_completion.py:28 (JOINT_MISSING 40, JOINT_INTRUSIONS 10, NOVEL_LINES 10, FRAC 0.90) |
| kill test bars | PASS only if at each of M = 250, 500, 1,000 on every gated seed the main arm (g = 0.3, J_fb*) meets: D1/2 joint >= 90 % of the 200 cued items; D3 novel half cue regenerates < 10 lines in total for >= 90 % of 200 novel cues; D4 joint criterion for >= 90 % of the oldest 100 items; D5 P2-E2's C1-C4 hold with bars unchanged | docs/plant2/P2-E3-content-completion.md:134-140 |
| verdict rules | every content criterion (D1/2, D3, D4) holds and only D5 fails on some seed = CONTENT PASS / P2-E2 REPLICATION FAIL; any content criterion fails = FAIL; shuffled-feedback arm must fail D1/2 at M = 1,000 on every gated seed else VOID; validity failure = INVALID. FAIL: stop this line, no parameter retuned | docs/plant2/P2-E3-content-completion.md:142-169; plant2/experiments/p2_e3_completion.py:394-419 |
| validity bars | forward store sha256 equals unmodified P2-E1 learning at every gated load; forward and feedback stores bit-identical before/after every test phase, feedback digest recorded at every load; mean eligible fraction >= 0.95; mean \|A\| in 18.5-21.5 at M = 1,000; convergence (P2-E2 rule) mean over cells of \|vbar change\| over last 10 s of each gated-load settle <= 0.2 mV, signed drift reported, M = 1,500 and 2,000 flagged never invalid | docs/plant2/P2-E3-content-completion.md:154-163 |
| calibration grid and rule | J_fb 1.00 to 6.00 mV step 0.05 (101 values) for g = 0.3 and the same 101 for g = 0 (202 arms from one raster per load); J passes if D1/2 joint, D3, D4 >= 0.90 at M = 250, 500, 1,000 (D5 reported, not in rule); J_fb* = midpoint of longest contiguous passing run, tie -> lower run, run touching 1.00 or 6.00 recorded censored and midpoint still taken; if no J passes at g = 0.3 P2-E3 FAILS and no gated seed runs; matched g = 0 arm same grid and rule, fallback = grid J with largest minimum over the pass fractions across loads, tie -> lower J | docs/plant2/P2-E3-content-completion.md:173-205 |

| loads | gated M = 250, 500, 1,000 (GATE_M); reported M = 1,500, 2,000 (REPORT_M); structural arms (label-permuted, plateau-set) and shuffled arm at M = 1,000 (STRUCT_M = 1000) | docs/plant2/P2-E3-content-completion.md:148-151, 182-188; plant2/experiments/p2_e3_completion.py:30-32 |

**Seeds.**

- exploration: Contract: 'Seeds 1-10 and 42-43 are not used' (:131-132; no reason given in the contract; cross-check: P2-E1 SEEDS 1-5 and P2-E2 GATED_SEEDS 6-10 / PAIRED_SEEDS 1-5 in p2_e1_btsp.py and p2_e2_accommodation.py, not named sources). Exploration seeds named in the record: 42 (contract critics' exploratory runs with the exact rec dynamics, 'not used here', :82-83; seed 43 also used by the critics, contract-redteam.md lines 7-10, 23, 36); 99 (north-star skeptic's structured-item script) and 97 (author's unchanged re-run) (P2-E3 :488-495); 42-43 (P2-E4 red-team Addendum 2, :612-617); review scripts' own seeds for the surrogate runs not stated. A unit test ran run_seed on gated seed 13 in an 800-cell configuration before gating ('No information can leak', :580-582); tests now use seeds 90 and up (:582).
- calibration: Seed 0 (held-out), contract :131-132. Used for the first calibration (code 8c240bc, digest 2583f5e1f7e30627), the re-run with fixed code (68beb4c, digest e829ac7011a8c2cd) and one re-read at the frozen values (J_fb* g 0.3; matched g 0 J; g 0 at J_fb*). Seed 0 is spent.
- gated: 11, 12, 13, 14, 15, all fresh (contract :131). Each run once at J_fb* = 2.80, J0 = 1.45, g = 0.3. Record order in the JSONL was 11, 14, 12, 15, 13 (two parallel processes). All five valid; all five PASS.
- spent or reserved: Spent: 0 (calibration), 11-15 (gated). Reserved at the time of the P2-E3 record: 16-20 untouched (DECISIONS.md:383-384; findings-code.md F9 'Seeds 16-20 are unused so far'; ledger). The owner's execution order assigns them to P2-E4: 'run it on untouched gated seeds 16-20' (DECISIONS.md:454; also EVAL-after-P2-E3.md:169-170, findings-code.md Next steps). Whether P2-E4 actually used them is not stated in the named sources; the STAGES.md P2-E4 row (outside the P2-E3 row) says FAIL 'on 5/5 fresh gated seeds' without naming them. Exploration seeds 42, 43, 97, 99 are exploratory only; 90+ reserved for tests after the review.
- notes: Calibration (seed 0) records exist for both the bugged and the fixed run; both stay in the append-only file (:294-295). Gated seeds were run once; no gated seed was re-run or replaced.

**Failures and errata.**

| what | kind | detail | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Config key collision: first calibration set window = 75 for the rec readout, but P2-E1/E2 memory criteria read the same key (should be 50 ms) | failed_implementation | Memory-layer D5 numbers in the first calibration (code 8c240bc, digest 2583f5e1f7e30627) were computed at 75 ms; seed 0 M = 1,000 C2 0.880. Found before any gated run; fix: separate rec_window key plus a test pinning both windows (68beb4c). All 202 readout arms bit-identical across the two runs; J* unchanged. Addendum item 6: the fix changed C2 0.880 -> 0.970, C3 0.970 -> 1.000, C4 spurious 0.90 -> 0.98 and flipped gate_ok False -> True on seed 0; looked for because D5 looked bad, using seed-0 diagnostics only; bug was in the test configuration, not the continuation. Both runs' records stay in the append-only file. | docs/plant2/P2-E3-content-completion.md:283-295, 529-546; findings-methodology.md F2 |
| readout.replay span counter silently returned zeros when cue spacing < SPAN (300 ms) or the raster ended early | failed_implementation | Latent defect (code review F1): with t_gap = 100 (spacing 200) total_span/item_span were 0 for 37 of 100 cues where brute force gave 75-80. The contract layout spaces cues exactly 300 ms, so no reported number was affected. Fixed after the result: one accumulator per cue, raises on overlapping scoring windows or a window past the raster (Addendum item 10; ledger row 96). | review/plant2/P2-E3/findings-code.md F1; docs/plant2/P2-E3-content-completion.md:583-591 |
| Label-permuted store is not degree-preserving as the contract states | failed_implementation | Contract: 'every per-cell and per-line degree is kept'. Reviewer's check (m=2400, n=1600, M=120): per-cell degree equal for 36 % of cells (corr 0.9996), per-line for 3.6 % of lines (corr 0.947); pi can have fixed points (none among cued items here). Reported arm only; reworded in the addendum. | review/plant2/P2-E3/findings-code.md F4; docs/plant2/P2-E3-content-completion.md:556-559 |
| Scoring path untested; committed slow test exercised E2 not E3; constant/config coupling (DIGEST omits scoring constants; cfg['rec_window'] not read at the time) | failed_implementation | Code review F2, F3, F6 (nit/minor): no live collision found beyond the window bug. Fixed or accepted in part after the result (new tests; window from cfg['rec_window']; P2-E3 digest unchanged, P2-E4 digest to cover every scoring constant). | review/plant2/P2-E3/findings-code.md F2, F3, F6; ledger rows 97, 98, 101 |
| Shuffled-feedback void check cannot fail; the gated protection check stores_unchanged_by_tests cannot fail | inadequate_experiment | Shuffled arm fails by construction (density 13 % below g); the label-permuted result follows from how a Willshaw store works; STAGES' 'against a never-trained or shuffled control' met in letter only. stores_unchanged_by_tests compares a test copy with itself. From P2-E4 on at least one gated control must be able to pass if the claim is false and main-line stores and vbar digested before/after every test phase. | docs/plant2/P2-E3-content-completion.md:563-570; findings-methodology.md F7; findings-code.md F7 |
| 'Inhibition required' was settled at calibration; no gated outcome could have read 'unnecessary' | inadequate_experiment | verdict() returns 'required' whenever the g = 0 arm uses the fallback J (--j0-fallback); the contract's two readings are not mutually exclusive when the fallback is used. Seed-0 margin was 0.885 against 0.90 (contract :302, :315; methodology F4 gives 0.88). Future contracts make the two readings mutually exclusive. | docs/plant2/P2-E3-content-completion.md:514-528; findings-methodology.md F4; plant2/experiments/p2_e3_completion.py:410-411 |
| Scope of the design: independent random items only, frozen copies after a 50 s settle, no generalisation tested | inadequate_experiment | Every gated item an independent random sparse pattern (about 2.5 % pairwise overlap); D3 requires novel cues to regenerate nothing so no generalisation could register; tests on discarded deep copies with plasticity off; protocol-scheduled episodes (quiet(), plateaus only in encodings, write at a protocol-set tick). 5/5 gated seeds resample the same iid distribution (skeptic NS5). | docs/plant2/P2-E3-content-completion.md:431-440, 483-503; findings-northstar_skeptic.md NS1, NS3, NS5 |

All adopted in the final contract; ledger rows 57-80 'accepted'. | review/plant2/P2-E3/contract-redteam.md lines 9-13, 36-38; review/ledger.jsonl rows 57-80 |
| Plateau-set control read backwards in the Result | interpretive_error_corrected | Result said 'The activity rule loses nothing' (:363) and 'The extra responders write extra feedback but cost no measurable specificity' (:393-395) and scored the plateau-set prediction as 'wrong: about equal' (:416). **Reproducibility.**

Commands:

```
python3 -m plant2.experiments.p2_e3_completion calibrate  (driver docstring, plant2/experiments/p2_e3_completion.py:8)
python3 -m plant2.experiments.p2_e3_completion run --J 2.6 --J0 1.45 --seeds 11 12 13 14 15 --gated --verdict  (driver docstring :9; the docstring's --J 2.6 is the pre-calibration prediction, the recorded gated J_star is 2.8 and J0 1.45, and the verdict record has j0_calibrated False, which corresponds to --j0-fallback in the argparse at :436; the exact command line used for the gated run is not recorded in the named sources)
python3 -m plant2.experiments.p2_e3_completion verdict --J 2.8 --J0 1.45 --j0-fallback  (argparse :426-429; verdict subcommand added between 68beb4c and c97f237)
git diff 68beb4c..c97f237 -- plant2  (contract addendum item 9: shows the 17-line verdict subcommand)
```

No simulation or test was run for this extraction.

**Conclusions.**

- Result (docs/plant2/P2-E3-content-completion.md:419-429): after one exposure per item and up to 1,000 items in 4,000 memory cells, a spiking network regenerates the actual missing half of an input from half of it: >= 80 % of missing features with fewer than 10 wrong ones for 95.5-100 % of items within 75 ms; median reconstruction error 2 of 50 lines; unlearned cues regenerate nothing (corrected to 'almost nothing'); what is regenerated is the specific content written for that memory. Needs the fixed activity-proportional inhibition at the higher load (scoped to M = 1,000 by Addendum item 5).
- Decision per contract (:442-443): Stage 1's content-completion clause is met; next an evaluation against the north star, then the online-memory experiment, then capacity.
- Review addendum corrected reading (:600-610): with independent random sparse items, up to 0.25 items per memory cell, and tests on frozen copies after a 50 s settle, the network stores each input verbatim against a random memory index and regenerates the missing half from the other half, with few intrusions. It is working plumbing for later stages (prediction, replay, comparison against input), not evidence of learning structure: untested on structured inputs (a labelled exploration says it fails), nothing reads the regenerated content, and past about 0.375 items per cell the store saturates instead of forgetting.
- Mechanism characterisation (Addendum item 2, :474-482): P2-E3 is a spiking implementation of Willshaw-style hetero-associative memory with random indices; the fixed inhibition implements the count threshold, about 30 % of the active memory cells; the label-permuted result follows because the output is whatever was filed under the key.
- The activity-based (responder) write adds nothing measurable over writing from the assigned plateau set and adds a few intrusions (Addendum item 1).
- EVAL-after-P2-E3.md:15-31: 'Not yet as evidence; yes as a piece of plumbing that later stages need.' Adds the first readout in input coordinates (23-28 ms, one-shot, few intrusions); does not add learning power; two threats: structured input (exploratory, 2 seeds) and no forgetting. Verdicts: Stage 2 necessary plumbing, sequence mechanism ahead; Stage 3 not advanced; Stages 4-5 distance unchanged.
- STAGES.md:83: PASS on 5/5 fresh gated seeds; meets Stage 1's content clause and unlocks no later gate; inhibition needed at M = 1,000 only; shuffled feedback 0.000 (a leak check); past 0.375 items per cell saturates rather than forgets.
- DECISIONS.md:355-363: four independent reviewers found no bug that changes a reported number; corrections appended with nothing above edited; readout span counter fixed and scoring tests added. DECISIONS.md:371-381: chose to stop before committing the P2-E4 contract and put five decisions to the owner; rejected committing P2-E4 as drafted and rejected starting a new mechanism to rescue the structured-input result ('unplanned tuning of a finding that has not yet been gated').
- Owner's rulings after the evaluation (DECISIONS.md:388-451, provenance: 'drafted with an outside assistant's help and relayed by the owner as their own decisions'): (1) structured-input robustness becomes a Stage 1 gate, P2-E3 PASS unchanged and scoped to independent random items; (2) P2-E4 stays focused on online memory, with beyond-capacity (M = 3,000) and no-write-oracle arms as reported stress tests; before freezing P2-E4 add a blank-cue control, a never-probed cohort of the oldest items, power checks for dependent measurements and the five-seed rule, and verification that the optimised replay matches continuous simulation (:412-419); (3) fixed inhibition in the Stage 2 memory layer conditionally approved, P2-E3's coefficient does not carry over; (4) resolve structured-memory contamination before Stage 2, error-correcting write an approved research direction not an assumed solution, familiarity-gated allocation deferred; (5) stronger Stage 3 gate (prototype effect jointly with exemplar specificity; superposition at retrieval does not count). Execution order (DECISIONS.md:453-457): red-team and freeze P2-E4 and run it on untouched gated seeds 16-20; structured-input diagnostic before any Stage 2 work, with a separately contracted correction first if it fails; capacity scaling continues, measuring content as well as index recall.

**Limitations.**

- Items were independent random patterns of one size (a = 100, 40 Hz, about 2.5 % pairwise overlap) with exactly-half cues; item size, cue fraction, rates and duration never varied (docs/plant2/P2-E3-content-completion.md:483-485; skeptic NS5).
- Tests ran on discarded deep copies with plasticity off after a 50 s settle; online learning, interference during learning and repeated-cue habituation are untested (:436-438); the settle matters (P2-E2 review: C1 0.875 straight after learning, 0.915 after 5 s, 0.94 after 50 s; findings-methodology.md F9).
- Regenerated content is the input copied verbatim (eligible lines) against a random plateau index; no generalisation, no abstraction; rec projects nowhere and nothing reads the regenerated content (:419-440, 474-482; findings-northstar_constructive.md F1).
- Capacity ends between 0.25 and 0.375 items per memory cell and more sharply for content than for the index; past it the add-only store saturates rather than forgets (:439-440, 504-513).
- The gated controls could not fail (shuffled arm by construction; stores_unchanged_by_tests compares a copy with itself); 'chance' is an overlap floor; label-permuted keeps degrees only approximately (:563-570, 554-562).
- 'Inhibition required' was settled at calibration and holds at M = 1,000 only; at M = 1,500 no inhibition beats the main arm (:514-528).
- J_fb* and the g = 0 fallback were calibrated on a single held-out seed (0) against the gated criteria at the gated loads; g = 0.3 was fixed by hand from prior exploration with different dynamics (same-tick inhibition) (:83-85, 192-205; skeptic NS5).
- The window-key bug fix changed the seed-0 D5 label (gate_ok False -> True) and was looked for because D5 looked bad (:539-544).
- Provenance: calibration and gated plant2 trees differ (17-line verdict subcommand); predictions committed about 7 s before the gated runs; some ledger stamps hand-entered and precede their commits; gated seed 13 used in a unit test on an 800-cell configuration (:571-582).
- Five of five gated seeds resample the same iid distribution, measuring seed variance rather than generalisation; each PASS was predicted within a few per cent by counting arithmetic (skeptic NS5).
- Exploratory figures (structured items on seeds 99 / 97, Addendum 2 on seeds 42-43, constructive surrogates and predictor runs, skeptic's 36-64 % overlap saturation) are labelled exploratory and their scripts and outputs are absent from this checkout.
- Addendum 2: P2-E3's content PASS is scoped to its own back-to-back (quiet()) learning protocol; the plateau-set write does not have this dependence (:636-641).
- Per-seed values of matched g = 0, g = 0 at J_fb*, plateau-set and C4 are given in the Result only as ranges or not at all; per-seed values quoted here from the JSONL are a cross-check, not part of the named sources.

**Open questions.**

- Does anything here learn from structure shared across experiences rather than filing each item under a random key? (skeptic, Next steps) Owner ruling DECISIONS.md:394-401: structured-input robustness (exemplars with about 16 % sibling overlap, M = 500 then 1,000, fresh seeds) becomes a Stage 1 gate.
- EVAL decision 1: should Stage 1's gate gain a predeclared clause for correlated items? Recommended yes. Owner: approved (DECISIONS.md:394-402).
- EVAL decision 2: P2-E4 scope past capacity and without the write oracle? Owner: approved with condition: gated below capacity; M = 3,000 and no-oracle arms as reported stress tests; bounded capacity and graceful forgetting required before any lifelong-memory claim (DECISIONS.md:403-419).
- EVAL decision 3: may Stage 2 declare fixed activity-proportional inhibition in the memory layer? Owner: conditionally approved; matched no-inhibition comparison, frozen parameters, independent controls; P2-E3's coefficient does not carry over (DECISIONS.md:420-429).
- EVAL decision 4: order of content-using mechanisms vs Stage 2? Owner: resolve structured-memory contamination before Stage 2; error-correcting write approved as research direction (one mechanism per contract); familiarity-gated allocation deferred (DECISIONS.md:430-443).
- EVAL decision 5: Stage 3 gate. Owner: approved a stronger gate (prototype effect jointly with exemplar specificity; label efficiency at predeclared hard distortion including about 0.8; superposition at retrieval does not count) (DECISIONS.md:444-451).
- Will recall hold online, while learning continues with no settle (interference, oldest-item retention, habituation and recovery)? Addendum 2 (exploratory) says the responder write may fail online; P2-E4 to measure; outcome is outside the P2-E3 sources.
- How does the content store forget gracefully (palimpsest) instead of saturating past about 0.375 items per cell? (skeptic NS4; DECISIONS.md:408-409)
- Does the P2-E3 operating point transfer to other item sizes (a = 50 and 200), other cue fractions (0.3), noisy cues and other rates? Untested (constructive F6; skeptic NS5).
- Does an error-correcting feedback write (potentiate missing lines, depress intrusions) supply useful error information and preserve unrelated memories? (EVAL-after-P2-E3.md:150-163; DECISIONS.md:435-439)
- Does fixed inhibition sit as an operating band around 0.25 items per cell on other seeds? Only M = 1,500 (not gated) shows g = 0 beating main.
- Does the J_fb window of 2.00-3.60 mV (seed 0) generalise across seeds? Not measured; gated seeds ran only at 2.80.
- Does capacity grow about linearly with cell count (Stage 1 clause, STAGES.md:45), and content fail first? Not tested in P2-E3; owner's capacity design is a separate experiment (DECISIONS.md:457; constructive Next steps).

**Experimenter supply remaining.**

- When to write: plateaus occur only inside protocol-marked encodings, so the system is told which inputs are new and which are tests; episodes begin with quiet() and the write fires once at a protocol-set tick (EVAL-after-P2-E3.md:44-46; skeptic NS3)
- The key: plateau cells drawn at random (f_q = 0.005) independent of content; a re-exposed item gets a second, unrelated memory (EVAL-after-P2-E3.md:47-48)
- The value: eligible input lines (>= 3 spikes in 200 ms) copied one-to-one onto the reconstruction layer by fixed 1:1 line-to-rec wiring (EVAL-after-P2-E3.md:49-50; contract :54-76)
- The operating point: J calibrated on a held-out seed (memory-layer window about +/-5 %), the inhibition ratio g fixed by hand, accommodation tracks mean input, recall needs rest before testing (EVAL-after-P2-E3.md:50-54)
- The input statistics: every gated item an independent random pattern of 100 lines at 40 Hz, cued with exactly half of its lines (EVAL-after-P2-E3.md:55-56)
- The test regime: tests on discarded copies with plasticity off, so recall never changes the system (EVAL-after-P2-E3.md:57-58)
- The 50 ms continuation after each 200 ms encoding and the rule that rec is not simulated during learning (contract :57-62, 51)
- Episode boundaries as an experimenter-provided write signal, to be removed before Stage 2 (findings-northstar_constructive.md F5)
- Owner-approved fixed element: activity-proportional inhibition on the readout (g = 0.3), with matched no-inhibition and shuffled-feedback comparisons required (contract :20-22; STAGES.md:93-95)

**Capabilities and hypotheses.** Capabilities claimed by this record are listed in RESEARCH-MAP.md; hypotheses in HYPOTHESES.md.

**Objective.** Test whether the P2-E2 + P2-E3 system (all parameters frozen: J = 1.525 mV, accommodation tau 10 s, J_fb = 2.80 mV, g = 0.3) keeps memory-assembly (index) recall and content recall for recent and old items while it learns one item every 500 ms with no settle and no quiet(), at loads M = 500 and 1,000 (within demonstrated capacity). Probe slots are interleaved into learning on one continuous timeline with a live reconstruction layer rec. Repeated-cue habituation and recovery are tested on copies with learning paused (sham encodings). The owner requires this test before Stage 1 counts as complete (docs/plant2/P2-E4-online-memory.md lines 3-25, 55-62). It removes only two experimenter supplies from the gated numbers: the rest before testing and the frozen-copy test regime (lines 80-81).

**Configuration**

**Seeds.**

- exploration: Seeds 42-43 only. These cover the red-team's rough re-implementations (labelled exploratory) and the real-driver exploration run (records from commit 737e8b1), from which the real-driver predictions were made, plus the exploration diagnosis D1-D4 (labelled exploratory). Non-gated seeds 90+ were used by the unit test that builds the schedule (contract line 220) and by reviewers' probes and checks: verify-schedule_power seeds 90-99; implementation and fix reviews seeds 90-99 (TINY seed 92; seeds 93 and 94; full-config seed 91; seed 90 replay; fix-check on TINY seeds 90 and 91); constructive results reviewer seed 90 (exploratory).
- calibration: None stated for P2-E4. Parameters are inherited frozen from P2-E2 and P2-E3: there is no new mechanism, and P2-E4 'removes only two experimenter supplies' (contract lines 83-88). The power reference uses recorded settled rates: for memory measures, P2-E2 seeds 6-10 pooled with P2-E3 seeds 11-15; for content measures (D3, joint, D4), P2-E3 seeds 11-15 alone (contract lines 387-389).
- gated: Seeds 16-20, all fresh ('Gated seeds 16-20 are untouched' at freeze), each run once on plant2 tree 9152f1e4 under digest f8f8838b. All five valid.
- spent or reserved: Gated 16-20: spent (5/5 recorded, valid, FAIL). Exploration 42-43: spent. Seeds 90+: used by tests and reviewer probes (non-gated). No gated seed is reserved for P2-E4; any P2-E4 re-run needs the owner's written ruling and keeps the first records. (P2-E5 uses gated 21-25 and exploration 44-45, outside this record.)
- notes: A gated seed runs once; a re-run needs the owner's ruling and keeps the first records (contract lines 378-379). The real-driver predictions commit 124ecb2 (00:38:40) precedes the first run-once marker (00:38:56). The owner's reading of 2026-10-11 (DECISIONS.md 09962a4) cited the first gated readings from lane logs before the records for seeds 16 and 17 existed; nothing changed afterwards (contract lines 798-800). The gated diagnosis (seeds 16-20) ran only after the verdict (e756252).

**Reproducibility.**

Commands:

```
python3 -m plant2.experiments.p2_e4_online explore --seeds 42 43
python3 -m plant2.experiments.p2_e4_online predict
python3 -m plant2.experiments.p2_e4_online run --seeds 16 17 18 19 20 --gated
python3 -m plant2.experiments.p2_e4_online verdict
python3 analysis/p2e4_diagnosis.py --seed 42 (and --seed 43, then the gated seeds 16-20 after the verdict; per the diagnosis plan)
python3 -m pytest -q plant2/tests -m "not slow" (plant2 fast suite, per AGENTS.md; not run here)
analysis/tests/test_p2e4_diagnosis.py (tests exhaustive D2 classes; not run here)
```

Artifacts:

- docs/plant2/P2-E4-online-memory.md (frozen contract cd17cde plus appended predictions, Result and readings)

- docs/plant2/EVAL-after-P2-E4.md
- docs/plant2/STAGES.md line 84
- plant2/experiments/p2_e4_online.py (CONTRACT dict lines 39-53; guard; CLI)
- plant2/power.py, plant2/tests/test_p2_e4.py
- analysis/p2e4_diagnosis.py, analysis/tests/test_p2e4_diagnosis.py
- bench/results/plant2.jsonl (append-only): power_reference, exploration_seed, power_predictions, kill_test_seed, kill_test_verdict, reported_arms, diagnosis records
- `review/plant2/P2-E4/*.md` (16 files: redteam-4, verify-3, impl-3, fixcheck-2, results-4) and review/ledger.jsonl rows 123-251 and 379-410
- Per-slot, habituation and main-continuation logs: gzip JSONL, with sha256_file and sha256_uncompressed recorded in each record, at `/root/.cache/brain-sim/plant2/p2_e4/seed{16..20,42,43}_{main,habituation,main_continuation}_<timestamp>.jsonl.gz` (21 logs: 15 gated, 6 exploration)

So the following cannot be re-checked here: the 21 hashed per-slot, habituation and main_continuation logs (gated seeds 16-20 and exploration seeds 42-43); the gated_seedN.started run-once markers; and the reviewers' exploratory artefacts (`~/.cache/brain-sim/review/p2e4-redteam/*`, and `~/.cache/brain-sim/review/p2e4-results/north-star/probe90.py`, probe90_sham.py, hab_margin2.py). Present: all repo-tracked documents, the append-only results records (hashes and per-seed vectors), and the review files. Independently of the missing markers, the guard in code also refuses a gated re-run when the seed is already in the recorded gated records.

Missing sources:

The gated_seedN.started run-once markers are also absent
