Caveats: Gated seeds run once. A re-run needs the owner's written ruling (--owner-ruled-rerun) and keeps the first records (contract lines 378-379), so a reproduction is not a new accepted attempt. Gated numbers come from a deterministic continuous simulation given the streams. The post-verdict diagnosis re-simulated the main timeline of seeds 16-20 with 0 mismatches over 1,000 logged steps per seed and D3_verified true, but it did NOT re-simulate the habituation copies; a replay of seed 18's M = 500 habituation copies is an optional reported-only check and was not run. Deterministic reproduction shows consistency with the recorded simulation, not proof of a causal explanation. The guard checks plant2/ cleanliness only, plant2 tree 9152f1e4 and digest f8f8838b. The habituation copy stream key (seed, 16, M, x + 1, s) uses x as the 0-based position in the drawn list of 50 (ledger row 220), whereas the contract text says item x. Duty-arm continuation counts were not persisted by the old driver (P2-R1 errata). Recorded runtime: numpy 2.5.3, python 3.13.16, x86_64. No simulation, test or script was run for this extraction; per-seed vectors, validity flags and the M250_rolling, efficiency, ood and wps values were read from bench/results/plant2.jsonl only.

Limitations and source disagreements:

- The experimenter still supplies the write oracle, the key, the value, the operating point and the input statistics; P2-E4 removes nothing at learning time (contract lines 64-81; Result lines 768-777).
- Habituation (O4, O5) is measured on copies with learning paused and sham encodings. Sham encodings hold input duty, load and memory-layer vbar but do not reproduce the post-write memory burst, so rec on the copies sits about 1.5-3 mV nearer rest at slot onset (verification SHAM-1; no measurable effect on 'both' in an n = 60 exploratory check) (contract lines 75-81).
- Under the contract's own minimum counts and power rule, O4 and O5 were not estimable at M = 1,000, nor O4 for seeds 16 and 20; the labelling rule absorbs these NOT ESTIMABLE conditions into FAIL labels (Result lines 660-661).
- The RECOVERY FAIL label depends on reading the power rule per seed and load (seeds = 1, loads = 1, recorded in ledger row 379); the all-seeds reading gives RECOVERY NOT ESTIMABLE; seed 18's M = 500 habituation copies were not replayed.
- Validity 5 holds by construction. Validity 4 depends on BinarySynapses.add()/toggle() always rebinding key arrays, so future plant2/btsp.py changes must be checked. Validity 9 has no content positive control, and no gated control for the recall claim can fail (contract lines 314-317, 348-349; results-code.md F1, F3).
- Twin B changes the presentation regime (50 s settle, separate input stream, P2-E3 rhythm, no learning episodes) as well as the state, so it shows state- or protocol-dependent recovery, not recovery specific to the threshold offset. The duty arm likewise changes the schedule as well as the offset (diagnosis plan lines 153-160; DECISIONS.md lines 496-497, 575-579).
- The trade-off (fast access vs clean content) is shown for the responder write, which recruits every cell above one shared threshold, not for the architecture. The P2-R1 errata say 'never both' overstates and that no tested regime meets the complete frozen all-seed contract (EVAL lines 33-35, 223-236).
- Post hoc and reviewer-derived numbers (C1 at 75 ms, recovery asymmetry 57 vs 2, graded habituation, content silence, the O4 null model, seed-90 burst-count probes) are labelled post hoc or exploratory and are not gates. C1's 50 ms window is frozen and the O1 failure stands.
- Predictions and power used exploration seeds 42-43 only (two seeds). The zero-online-cost full-rule P(PASS) with P2-E3-only memory rates, 0.768-0.786, sat just below the 0.8 target, as stated before freezing (contract lines 432-434).
- Blocks span loads (261-500 and 761-1,000) rather than a single M, and the contract states the gated loads as ranges. M = 250 is not gated (reported from rolling probes, n 6-16 per seed per the records).
- SOURCE DISAGREEMENT (cohort and block counts): review/ledger.jsonl rows 123, 127 and 158 describe the revised but unfrozen contract (items 1-160, two cohorts of 80; full cues cut to 10, novel to 30). The frozen contract docs/plant2/P2-E4-online-memory.md lines 96, 162-172, 190-199 states items 1-200, two cohorts of 100, novel 20, and no full cues in gated blocks. Ledger row 167 and the commit cd17cde message record the enlargement. The frozen text governs.
- SOURCE DISAGREEMENT (power headline): ledger row 127 says O1-O3 together 0.83 over all seeds and loads (revised contract) while the frozen contract lines 402-406 give 0.855 (pooled memory rates), 0.788 (P2-E3-only), 0.62 (earlier draft).
- SOURCE DISAGREEMENT (seed 18 M = 500 recovery p0): docs/plant2/P2-E4-online-memory.md gives 0.83 in the table and an exact value of 0.831 in the text (lines 643, 677-678); review/plant2/P2-E4/results-methodology.md F1 gives 0.829 (Monte Carlo across seeds ~0.83); results-code.md F2 gives driver Monte Carlo 0.8289 and exact beta-binomial 0.831.
- SOURCE DISAGREEMENT (control-copy rates): Result lines 692-694 'realised control rates (0.81-0.85, not the 0.963 assumed)'; results-methodology.md F6 'observed control late rate was 0.75-0.82 at M=500'; results-northstar_skeptic.md NS-6 'control copies pooled both rate is 0.61-0.65' at M = 1,000; contract line 565 exploration control-copy 'both' rate 0.799 / 0.586.
- SOURCE DISAGREEMENT (range scopes): lures recalled as the stored item 0.90-0.95 (Result line 730, gated seeds) vs 0.7-0.95 (results-northstar_skeptic.md NS-4, seven seeds including exploration); writes-during-probes stored fraction 0.165-0.20 (Result line 727) vs 0.165-0.21 (skeptic) vs 0.16-0.20 (constructive NS7); |R| 41-44.5 (Result line 746; skeptic NS-3 41.2-44.5) vs 41-45 (EVAL line 88; constructive NS1).
- SOURCE DISAGREEMENT (habituation stream key): contract line 141 says '(seed, 16, M, x + 1, s) ... for item x', while the driver at plant2/experiments/p2_e4_online.py line 583 (line 568 at the reviewed commit, per impl-recompute.md R3) calls stream(S.seed, HAB, M, i + 1, s) with i the 0-based list position (impl-recompute.md R3, impl-conformance.md N-1). Ledger row 220 records this reading. The two are statistically equivalent, and validity 8 holds either way.
- SOURCE DISAGREEMENT (label prediction): EVAL-after-P2-E4.md line 21 says 'P2-E4 failed as predicted', while the real-driver label probabilities (contract lines 588-600) put RECOVERY NOT ESTIMABLE at 0.57 and RECOVERY FAIL at 0.36, and the pre-implementation expected labels (lines 503-504) were HABITUATION NOT ESTIMABLE and RECOVERY NOT ESTIMABLE at M = 1,000.
- SOURCE DISAGREEMENT ('never both'): EVAL-after-P2-E4.md lines 27-28 and 29-31 and STAGES line 84 say that with one state at M = 1,000 the system gives fast access or clean content, not both. The P2-R1 errata (EVAL lines 227-229) say 'Never both' overstates (seed 18 settled twin B index 0.955, content joint 0.915). The original verdicts stand.
- SOURCE DISAGREEMENT (skeptic NS-2 vs Result): review/plant2/P2-E4/results-northstar_skeptic.md NS-2 recommends stating the usable online load lies between 0.125 and 0.25 items per cell; ledger row 394 does not adopt it because the main line fails online index at M = 500 too.
- SOURCE DISAGREEMENT (O4 wording): the results-northstar_skeptic.md verdict says 'O4 fails estimably at M = 500', while the Result vector says O4 is not estimable on seeds 16 and 20 (eligible 21, 20) and estimable on seeds 17-19 only (lines 655-656).
- SOURCE DISAGREEMENT (stress joint at M = 3,000): Result line 723 gives 0.00; results-northstar_skeptic.md NS-7 gives 'joint 0.0-0.01 at M = 3,000 on every seed'.
- SOURCE DISAGREEMENT ('failure to store'): the owner's reading says the high-load failure 'is not necessarily a failure to store' (DECISIONS.md line 606), while the closeout entry says 'The failure at high load is not a failure to store' (DECISIONS.md line 716).
- SOURCE DISAGREEMENT (P2-E5 exploration figures cited in the P2-E4 evaluation): the plateau-set store's grid best at M = 1,000 is 'only 0.665-0.69' in EVAL-after-P2-E4.md line 160 vs '0.60-0.665' in results-northstar_constructive.md NS8; the continuation-count AUC is 'AUC >= 0.996' in DECISIONS.md line 622 vs '0.9994-0.9995 at M = 500; 0.995 at M = 1,000' in EVAL line 128.

**Conclusions.**

- P2-E4 FAILED as a gate: ONLINE INDEX FAIL + HABITUATION FAIL + RECOVERY FAIL on 5 of 5 valid fresh gated seeds (16-20), each run once on frozen code. Two reviewers independently re-derived the result from sha256-matched logs (results-code.md, results-methodology.md).
- P2-E4 adds no capability and removes no supply at learning time ('P2-E4 adds no capability and removes no supply at learning time. Its value is a supply nobody had named.'). 'Online' means recall while scheduled learning continues, not online learning.
- Online index recall within 50 ms is 0.87-0.93 at M = 500 and 0.68-0.74 at M = 1,000, including the newest items. The failures are mostly slowed retrievals of intact assemblies (post hoc: 0.89-0.93 of failing cues reach the bar within 75 ms; C1 at 75 ms is about 0.955-0.97, recall only). 86-100 % of failing cues pass after a settle on unchanged synapses (state- or protocol-dependent recovery, not erasure).
- Online content (O2, O3 content) passes on every seed and load, but at M = 1,000 only in the online state. The online-written store is contaminated (twin A 0.82-0.905, against 0.985-1.0 for the plateau-set store on the same raster), and content reads worse after a settle (0.835-0.915 against 0.93-0.965 online).
- Newly named experimenter supply: separate encoding and retrieval states set by the schedule. P2-E3's PASS wrote at a high continuation offset (8.35-8.56 mV at items 751-1,000) and read after a 50 s settle (3.65-3.74 mV). With one state at M = 1,000 the system gave fast access or clean content, not both on the same synapses; this is shown for the responder write, not for the architecture. P2-R1 errata (2026-10-12): 'never both' overstates, since seed 18's settled twin B has index 0.955 and content 0.915.
- Input duty, not recall, drives the contamination (reported arm, not gated: the novel-duty twin never retrieves yet writes the same store quality). Which state variable (the offset or another schedule difference) is responsible is not isolated; the gated data do not replicate the exploration's offset correlate.
- Post hoc reading, not a gate: habituation is graded and universal, since every repeatedly driven assembly accommodates (mean A(x) recall drop 0.107-0.134; net assembly offset rise 2.0-2.1 mV), and O4 counts the items near the bar. Recovery of the repeated item's own cues is incomplete (post hoc, 57 vs 2) while collateral cues are spared. The RECOVERY FAIL label itself rests on one seed and load, one item short.
- Learning outside the write oracle mostly fails (reported arms, not gated): writes during probes are 0.165-0.20 recallable; 30 % cues give C1 0.00; noisy half cues 0.075-0.175; lures sharing 50 lines are recalled as the stored item 0.90-0.95. Past capacity the store blacks out (stress joint 0.00 at M = 3,000; skeptic 0.0-0.01).
- Next step per the record: proceed to P2-E5 as the owner ordered, with no mechanism contract before P2-E5's verdict and the owner's rulings. The candidate next contract proposed to the owner is burst-gated feedback eligibility (>= 3 continuation spikes, k fixed by P2-E5 Part C). It touches only the content write and would inherit every index failure by construction. Changing the accommodation time constant is not the fix (owner, 2026-10-11). The burst (activity) form comes from the constructive reviewer, not the owner: the owner's own candidate is a local eligibility signal tied to a genuine plateau event, and in the current model a plateau-trace-gated write reproduces the assigned-assembly store exactly and inherits the random, content-blind key (proposed, untested; DECISIONS.md lines 590-598, 624-625; EVAL-after-P2-E4.md lines 110-118).
- Takeover supplement (STAGES.md lines 112-120): P2-R1 reported a burst-write replay on reused seeds 44/90. R3 improves settled duty1000 content from .205 to .995 and beats count-matched random, but correlated1000 content remains .160 with index specificity .500. Supplied plateau keys remain; there is no live R3 and no new gated capability. No Stage 2 work began.

**Open questions.**

- Owner decision 1 (EVAL-after-P2-E4.md lines 207-210): may burst-gated feedback eligibility (>= 3 continuation spikes, k = 3 from P2-E5 Part C) be the next contract after P2-E5's verdict? It would gate only the online-written store's twin A joint at M = 500 and 1,000 on the main line and the duty arm, plus P2-E3's content gates, on fresh seeds. The owner's answer is not in the listed sources
- Owner decision 2: may fixed, activity-proportional stabilising inhibition in the memory layer join a Stage 1 recurrent completion mechanism (ruling 3 allowed it for Stage 2 only)
- Owner decision 3: where does forward-index cross-talk on correlated items go, before or after the access mechanism (P2-E5 Part B informs it)
- Owner decision 4: should future contracts use one latency bar for both index (50 ms) and content (75 ms), set before data and tied to a downstream need such as a Stage 2 chaining period
- Owner decision 5 (optional): replay seed 18's M = 500 habituation copies as a reported-only check, since RECOVERY FAIL rests on that seed and load alone
- Whether the offset alone, or another schedule difference (rhythm, input stream, learning episodes), is responsible for the encoding-retrieval trade-off; needs a separately contracted intervention (not run)
- What distinguishes slowed-retrieval cues at the cue level (per-item drive margin not measured; onset offset does not distinguish them in the gated data)
- Whether the trade-off belongs to the write rule or the architecture. The decisive reading is the duty arm's twin A joint (0.175-0.245) and online joint (0.485-0.565) under a burst-gated write. Duty-arm continuation counts were not persisted, so an instrumented reproduction is needed (P2-R1 errata)
- Association between online index failure and habituation (not estimable: median 0 online probes per habituation item)
- Spurious cells and ignition at 75 ms were not measured, so C1 at 75 ms about 0.955-0.97 covers recall only
- Whether feedback changes would alter the O4/O5 composite labels (1009/50000 repeated presentations pass index but not the composite); the criteria must be rescored if feedback changes (P2-R1 errata)
- Reported-arm outcomes not stated in the Result narrative: probed vs never-probed difference, retention-vs-age slope detail, cued-minus-blank by age beyond methodology F8
- Whether the recovery shortfall (57 vs 2 post hoc) is a real effect of repetition; would need a predeclared contract (the label rests on one seed and load)

**Experimenter supply remaining.**

- When to write (the write oracle): plateaus and feedback writes happen only inside scheduled encodings (200 ms encoding, 50 ms continuation); the experimenter sets when and for how long to write
- The key: plateaus are random and content-blind (they define A(x))
- The value: eligible lines are copied one to one onto rec
- The operating point: J, J_fb, g and accommodation tau are frozen; the operating point depends on input duty (50 %); the responder write's specificity depends on the operating point at write time
- The input statistics: items are independent random patterns, with clean 50-line half cues, fixed readout windows (50 ms index, 75 ms content) and item boundaries; correlated items are P2-E5's subject
- NEW (named by P2-E4): separate encoding and retrieval states, set by the schedule. P2-E3's PASS relied on writing at a high continuation offset (8.35-8.56 mV at items 751-1,000) and reading after a 50 s settle (3.65-3.74 mV). With one state at M = 1,000 the system gives fast access or clean content, not both (shown for the responder write, not for the architecture)
- The gaps separating encoding from probes (50 ms pre-gap, 100 ms post-gap) and the exposure length of a probe slot (100 ms vs 200 ms encoding; per skeptic NS-4, storage depends on it)
- The test regime still supplied for habituation: copies with learning paused and sham encodings (P2-E4 removes the frozen-copy test regime only for the main line)
- Removed by P2-E4 for the gated numbers: the rest before testing, and the frozen-copy test regime on the main line (items 4 and 6 of the EVAL supply list, partly); EVAL supply table row 4 'the rest before testing is removed for the test; it fails without it'

**Capabilities and hypotheses.** This record demonstrates no new capability ('P2-E4 adds no capability and removes no supply at learning time', docs/plant2/EVAL-after-P2-E4.md lines 16-19, 55; docs/plant2/P2-E4-online-memory.md lines 768-771). Its gated passes (online content O2/O3, C2, C3, D3) and its reported-arm and post hoc readings enter RESEARCH-MAP.md only with the scope conditions written beside them. Its hypotheses are in HYPOTHESES.md.

**Objective.** Run the existing P2-E3 system with no new mechanism and one changed variable (item distribution: independent random items replaced by exemplars of F = 10 hidden prototypes, s = 60 lines replaced per exemplar, about 17 % sibling overlap) as a Stage 1 gate at M = 500 and 1,000 on fresh gated seeds 21-25, settled P2-E3 protocol (docs/plant2/P2-E5-structured-items.md L23-38, L162-175, L260-262). Hypothesis, expected to FAIL: the frozen system stores correlated exemplars item-specifically, with S1-S4 holding on every gated seed at both loads (L107-117). Reported Parts A-G decide which failure is most consequential (contamination, index, operating point) and what goes to the owner before any mechanism contract (L337-567, L675-720). Generalisation to never-seen family members is not measured here (L102-105).

| gate bars | S1 = P2-E2 C1-C4 as P2-E3's D5: C1 recall >= 0.8 for >= 90 % of 200 half cues; C2 spurious < 0.5\|A\| for >= 90 %; C3 novel ignition < 0.5 mean\|A\| for >= 90 % of 200 novel cues; C4 C1 and C2 within oldest 100. S2 = P2-E3 joint (>= 40 of 50 missing lines regenerated, < 10 intrusions, 75 ms window) for >= 90 % of 200 half cues. S3 = D3 (< 10 lines) for >= 90 % of 200 novel cues. S4 = joint for >= 90 % of 100 oldest items. Result states bars as C2 180 of 200, C4 spurious 90 of 100, S2 180 of 200, S4 90 of 100. | docs/plant2/P2-E5-structured-items.md L264-272, L894-895 |
| validity checks 1-7 | 1 forward-store sha256 equals plain P2-E1 on same items; 2 test copies leave stores bit-identical (a) and main line unchanged (b); 3 mean eligible fraction >= 0.95 and mean \|A\| 18.5-21.5 at M=1,000; 4 convergence mean \|dvbar\| change over last 10 s of settle <= 0.2 mV (signed drift reported; draft signed 0.1 mV rule withdrawn); 5 shuffled feedback joint < 0.90 at M=1,000 (INVALID on failure; leak check that cannot fail on this distribution); 6 realised mean \|x intersect y\| over within-family pairs 15-19 lines (driver overlap_lo 15.0, overlap_hi 19.0); 7 R(x) = cells with >= 1 captured continuation spike, feedback store = union of R(x) x E(x), R_1 store sha256 equals main store | docs/plant2/P2-E5-structured-items.md L289-317; driver L49 |
| readout grid and Part B/C/D constants | Grid J_fb in {1.5,2.0,2.4,2.8,3.2,3.6,4.0,5.0,6.0} x g in {0,0.3,0.4,0.5,0.6,0.7,0.8,1.0} = 72 points; grid best = maximum joint, first maximum in grid order, no D3/D4 condition, labelled optimistic. R_k for k = 2,3,4,6 (R_1 = main). Part B: losses in cues, L_c capped at 180, material >= 20 cues, dominance margin >= 10 cues, >= 4 attributable seeds. Part C: count AUC >= 0.95 and J(R_3) >= J(plateau-set) - 10 cues. Part D: R = (median intrusions online + 1)/(median P2-E3-protocol + 1); WORSENS R >= 1.25, NOT WORSE R < 1.10 on >= 4 valid seeds; AT FLOOR when P2-E3-protocol joint < 20 cues. Driver CONTRACT dict: frac 0.90, material_frac 0.10, margin_frac 0.05, worsens_ratio 1.25, not_worse_ratio 1.10, min_seeds 4, auc_bar 0.95, n_newex 100, n_proto 50, win_mem 50. | docs/plant2/P2-E5-structured-items.md L362-375, L377-419, L421-443, L467-480; driver L44-51 |
| guard and run-once | --gated refuses unless plant2/ committed, contract unchanged except appended lines, frozen commit is an ancestor of HEAD, and HEAD's results file holds a P2-E5 power_predictions record with this contract digest and plant2 tree; run-once markers in ~/.cache/brain-sim/plant2/p2_e5/; each gated seed runs once | docs/plant2/P2-E5-structured-items.md L334-335, L746-752; driver L1213-1241 |

**Seeds.**

- exploration: Seeds 44-45: red-team's and verifiers' scripts (exploratory numbers; contract L16-17, L744-745), then the real driver's explore run (power_predictions record 05:06:53Z, digest 8ab1cb47ff6ac06e, plant2 tree dc16f182, 400 posterior draws, exploration records committed up to 8c99564), and fix-check R1 on P2-E4's frozen driver (seeds 44-45, M = 500, exploratory). Verifier seeds 90-92 gave the generator overlap statistic (L185-186); unit tests use seeds 90 and up (L759).
- calibration: None. 'No calibration: every value is frozen from P2-E2 and P2-E3.' (contract L263). The 72-point J_fb x g grid is a reported readout diagnostic, not calibration; a later correction that changes store density must recalibrate on a held-out seed by P2-E3's rule or argue its frozen readout (L711-712).
- gated: 21, 22, 23, 24, 25: all fresh, each runs once; gated condition s = 60, F = 10, M = 500 and 1,000 (L260-262). Result: 5 of 5 valid (L869).
- spent or reserved: Gated 21-25 spent (each run once; verdict f25d3ed); a re-run needs the owner's ruling and keeps the first records (L334-335, L718). Exploration 44-45 spent. Seeds 90+ used by tests and verifier. STAGES L116 records P2-R1 replay on 'mandatory reused seeds 44/90' (not a P2-E5 reservation).
- notes: Timeline per Result L880-884: predict wrote power_predictions at 05:06:53Z; container restarted (boot 14:02:22) before that record was committed (694038c, 14:03:14); run-once markers for seeds 21 and 24 dated 14:03:24 and 14:03:26; no cache file dated between 05:07 and 14:03; so no gated run preceded the predictions commit and the guard held (methodology review m2 corrected an earlier misdescription of this sequence, review/plant2/P2-E5/results-methodology.md L41-45). Code reading (not stated in named docs): driver L1272-1275 refuses any seed already in committed kill_test_seed records even with --owner-ruled-rerun, which bypasses only the marker.

**Reproducibility.**

Commands:

```
python3 -m plant2.experiments.p2_e5_structured explore --seeds 44 45
python3 -m plant2.experiments.p2_e5_structured predict
python3 -m plant2.experiments.p2_e5_structured run --seeds 21 22 23 24 25 --gated
python3 -m plant2.experiments.p2_e5_structured verdict
analysis/p2e5_power.py (power reference; --append writes the power_reference record; final check ran it read-only without --append)
python3 -m pytest -q plant2/tests -m "not slow" (generic plant2 fast suite from AGENTS.md; P2-E5 tests are plant2/tests/test_p2_e5.py)
```

Artifacts:

- docs/plant2/P2-E5-structured-items.md (contract, predictions, Result)
- docs/plant2/EVAL-after-P2-E5.md
- plant2/experiments/p2_e5_structured.py
- plant2/tests/test_p2_e5.py
- analysis/p2e5_power.py
- bench/results/plant2.jsonl (power_reference, exploration_seed, power_predictions, kill_test_seed, reported_arm, kill_test_verdict records; present in this checkout)
- review/plant2/P2-E5/ (redteam-*, verify-*, fixcheck-*, fixcheck2-*, impl-*, results-*, finalcheck)
- review/ledger.jsonl (157 rows whose review field names P2-E5)
- persistence files per gated seed: gzip of an uncompressed npz (E, A, R, continuation counts and first-spike ticks, learning raster, test raster, per-cue outcomes) with sha256 in the record, intended at ~/.cache/brain-sim/plant2/p2_e5/

Caches present or missing: Checked on this host: `~/.cache/brain-sim/` has no plant2 directory, so the p2_e5 persistence files and gated_seed{21..25}.started run-once markers are absent; `~/.cache/brain-sim/review/` is also absent (reviewers' scratch scripts such as p2e5-results/code-correctness/{recompute,indep,replaycheck}.py, p2e5-final/ outputs). The EVAL errata states 'All 28 referenced old cache paths are absent here.' Per-cue records remain in bench/results/plant2.jsonl reported_arm records (200 per-cue joint and index outcomes per load per P2-E3-protocol arm).

Caveats: A re-run is not a new accepted attempt: each gated seed runs once and a re-run needs the owner's ruling and keeps the first records (contract L334-335, L718); code reading shows the driver refuses seeds already in committed kill_test_seed records even with --owner-ruled-rerun (driver L1272-1275). Exact same-raster replay depends on the missing persisted rasters; items x are not persisted and must be regenerated from streams (seed,20,F) and (seed,21,F,k) and checked against E coverage (CC-4). Not every recommended replay input was persisted (E4 duty and E5 online need new capture in faithful inherited-driver reproductions, EVAL errata L140-141). The plant2 tree must be dc16f182 and reused P2-E4 files must match blobs in tree 9152f1e4; cost about 45-55 min per seed unloaded, 1.5-2 h under two-lane contention. I ran nothing in this extraction (read-only).

**Conclusions.**

- P2-E5 failed as predeclared: STRUCTURED INDEX FAIL + STRUCTURED CONTENT FAIL on 5 of 5 valid gated seeds; Stage 1's structured-input clause is not met; P2-E3's PASS stands scoped to independent items and its protocol (contract L869-871).
- Content fails on every seed at both loads (S2 0.095-0.18 at M=500, 0.0-0.005 at M=1,000) while the same system on independent items (s = 100, reported arm) replicates P2-E3 (joint 0.97-1.0) (contract L909, L922).
- The memory index fails on correlated items: narrow at M=500 (2 of 5 seeds, 2-6 cues short; 3 of 5 seeds pass S1) and broad at M=1,000 (C2 0.435-0.515); the memory raster is independent of the feedback store, so no feedback-write correction can change C1-C4 (contract L905-911).
- Part B (reported-only) reads CONTAMINATION-DOMINANT at both loads under the predeclared grid-best convention; at the frozen readout M=1,000 would read INDEX-DOMINANT on 5 of 5 seeds; at M=500 L_i = 0 does not mean the index is sound (contract L915-918, L927-931).
- At M=1,000 no tested store, including the plateau-set store and the activity-written R_3 store, reaches S2 on these rasters (same-raster replay, not a gated test); line load on shared prototype lines (F = 10) contributes; the clause does not fix F (contract L932-937, L994-996).
- Part C (reported-only): cells firing >= 3 continuation spikes recover the experimenter's plateau key (count AUC 0.9988-0.9995 at M=500, 0.9955-0.9971 at M=1,000); R_3 matches the plateau-set store within 0-7 cues; a measurement, not a mechanism, and removes no supply (contract L939-947).
- Part D (reported-only, not gated) reads WORSENS at both loads (R 1.66-1.95, 1.69-1.84): the store written on P2-E4's online schedule carries more intrusions than P2-E3's protocol store, as a whole-regime comparison measured at saturation at M=1,000; online the memory layer fires less (twin B memory loses 12-30 cues at M=500 and 65-79 at M=1,000; content gains 84-103 and 8-13 cues) (contract L948-953, L971-977).
- P2-E5 removes no experimenter supply and changes one (item distribution) (contract L1022-1023).
- Decision as predeclared: nothing retuned, no Stage 2 work, familiarity-gated allocation deferred; the index failure, the M=1,000 convention disagreement, the finding that no write-only correction reaches S2 at M=1,000, the choice of F and the pending EVAL-after-P2-E4 questions go to the owner (contract L1012-1021).
- Subsequent owner ruling recorded in the errata (2026-10-12): frozen readout primary; F10 canonical; R3 reported replay first; forward-index mechanism next (docs/plant2/EVAL-after-P2-E5.md L144-145). STAGES takeover table (2026-10-12) then lists: P2-R1 reported burst-write replay (correlated1000 content remains .160, index specificity .500; no live R3 or new gated capability); P2-E6 presynaptic-history encoding valid FEASIBILITY FAIL on seed101; P2-R2 formal two-seed result INVALID (docs/plant2/STAGES.md L112-124).

**Limitations.**

- P2-E5 is a diagnostic of the existing system, not a step that reduces experimenter-supplied cognition; it removes none of the supplied items and adds one, the item distribution (contract L159-160, L1022-1023).
- M=500 index failure rests on 2 of 5 seeds by 2-6 cues; the label stands under the frozen every-seed rule but is not robust (contract L905-907).
- Part B at M=1,000 depends on the grid-best convention; grid bests are optimistic (selected on the same data) and edge-censored on some seed-loads (plateau-set at J 6.0; main store at g 1.0); L_o is measured at P2-E4's frozen readout so losses mix conventions (contract L927-935; skeptic NS1).
- Part C is scored against the experimenter-assigned A(x); k = 3 fixed in advance; R_4 and R_6 must not be chosen post hoc (contract L939-947).
- Part D compares whole regimes (schedule, probes, offset not separated); AT FLOOR at M=1,000 and seed 25 at M=500; D1 near 1 at M=1,000 is uninformative (permuted store regenerates 0.535-0.583 item-specific lines) (contract L948-956).
- Online vs settled C2 comparison is unpaired; no 75 ms memory rate or latency was measured for the online arm; paired per-cue C1 x C2 analysis would be needed (contract L971-977).
- Pooled control does not isolate sibling organization from the marginal load distribution; 'correlation-attributable' is a frozen label, not clean causal attribution; F = 10 largely sets the content ceiling at M=1,000 and the clause does not fix F (EVAL errata L127-131; contract L994-996).
- S3 and the shuffled leak check cannot detect family contamination on this distribution; no gated control that could pass if the claim were false (contract L270, L279-287).
- New-exemplar and prototype cues measure index cross-talk and retrieval-time superposition, not generalisation; they count for nothing as learned abstraction (contract L548-567).
- Results hold for s = 60, F = 10, the settled protocol, 5 gated seeds each run once; online arm uses P2-E4's frozen driver (reported, not gated). P(PASS) numerically zero is not proof that a pass is impossible (contract L807-810, L979-981).
- Items x and not all replay inputs are persisted; replay requires regeneration from streams 20 and 21; E4 duty and E5 online need new capture; persistence caches absent on this host (results-code CC-4; EVAL errata L140-141).
- Part F per-line rates for earlier-only and both classes are biased about 5 % low; recompute with empty classes excluded before quoting (results-code CC-1).
- Exploratory numbers rest on seeds 44-45, and some on one seed or one lens; labelled exploratory in the contract (L16-17).
- Seven gated records have git dirty true outside plant2/ (other lane's uncommitted appends); plant2 tree identical on every record (contract L877-879).

**Open questions.**

- Burst-gated eligibility (R_3): gate it now or run it as a reported replay only (EVAL recommends replay); on repeats and lures old cells would write new content, colliding with the deferred question of context identity (EVAL Decision 1). Owner ruling recorded afterward: 'R3 reported replay first' (EVAL L144-145).
- Stabilising inhibition in the memory layer for a Stage 1 recurrent mechanism (EVAL Decision 2, EVAL-after-P2-E4 Q2); a recurrent completion basin must beat the no-recurrence system's own threshold curve (constructive NS4, deferred).
- Forward-index cross-talk: may the next gated contract target it; choose between error-gated eligibility and presynaptic-load gating by a predeclared contrast (EVAL Decision 3). Owner ruling afterward: 'forward-index mechanism next'.
- One latency bar for index and content (EVAL Decision 4, unchanged).
- Which F should the structured clause assume (F = 10: plateau-set 0.14-0.175 vs F = 40: 0.83-0.91 at M=1,000) (EVAL Decision 5). Owner ruling afterward: 'F10 canonical'.
- Which convention ranks losses in future contracts (grid-best vs frozen readout at M=1,000) (EVAL Decision 6). Owner ruling afterward: 'frozen readout primary'.
- Which online state variable (threshold offset, schedule, probes) causes the retrieval and contamination differences; a paired per-cue C1 x C2 analysis on the same cues is needed.
- Whether the M=500 index failure is robust or an artefact of 2 of 5 seeds being 2-6 cues short.
- Optional from EVAL-after-P2-E4: a reported replay of P2-E4 seed 18's habituation copies (EVAL L107).
- Whether a write-only correction could reach S2 at M=1,000 outside the tested stores and readout points (errata: 'no store' means none of those tested).

**Experimenter supply remaining.**

- When to write (the write oracle): plateaus and feedback writes happen only inside protocol-marked encodings.
- The key: plateaus are random and content-blind.
- The value: eligible lines are copied one to one onto rec.
- The operating point: J, J_fb, g and accommodation tau are frozen; the settled protocol is imposed (and P2-E4 added that P2-E3's pass relied on separate encoding and retrieval states set by the schedule, STAGES L84).
- The input statistics (new here): hidden prototypes, the overlap s, the number of families F, and the cyclic family order.
- P2-E5 removes none of these and adds one, the item distribution; EVAL-after-P2-E4.md items 1-7 are stated unchanged (not read in this extraction).

**Capabilities and hypotheses.** This record demonstrates no capability: the gated arm failed (FAIL on 5 of 5 valid seeds), and every positive finding is a reported arm or same-raster replay, so nothing from P2-E5 enters RESEARCH-MAP.md's capability table. The capability-like findings in the extraction stay in this entry's Controls and Results rows, labelled reported-only: the s = 100 arm replicates P2-E3 on independent items (contract L922, L987), s = 80 passes content at M = 500 (L988, L990), the plateau key is recoverable from continuation activity (L939-947), and the assigned-A plateau-set store completes content at M = 500 (L355-357, L932-937; a diagnostic comparator, not a capability of the system). Hypotheses are in HYPOTHESES.md.

**Objective.** Three predeclared questions (docs/plant2/P2-R1-takeover-replay.md:8-10): (1) Do committed P2-E4/E5 counts and verdicts support the reported failures? (2) Does R_3 improve content on unchanged memory rasters beyond merely reducing writer count? (3) Does this hold in the longer-gap duty regime and where correlated inputs damage the index? Method: audit all available aggregate records and raw-artifact availability ('Do not call an aggregate check a raw-data replication', contract:13-14), then reproduce selected exploratory timelines with the inherited engine and replay five feedback-writer stores on one shared settled memory raster per load (contract:12-39). Duty line is 'a store-quality diagnostic, not evidence that all online requirements pass' (contract:24-25). Contract end-state: 'Use the measured result to select one forward-index mechanism; do not tune R_3' (contract:55).

| Baseline audited | baseline commit 55e16ebff62a90d6402745f44fb860010741eba5, source_blob fc757b171de7e85e3a67bddd13ef5e218cee33d1 (bench/results/plant2.jsonl at that commit); no experiment driver imported or executed by the audit; scope 'Aggregate checks and independent input regeneration; no raw-data replication or new experiment.' | bench/results/p2r1-audit.jsonl:1; analysis/p2r1_baseline_audit.py:1-4,184 |
| Owner rulings governing the run | (1) R_3 reported replay first; compare inherited, assigned, size-matched random, anti-selected writers, including duty schedule and E5 correlations; recovering the supplied plateau key is not self-selected representation. (2) Fixed activity-proportional memory inhibition authorized as a Stage 1 stabilizer with controls; 'It is not learning' (not used in P2-R1). (3) Forward-index interference is the next primary mechanism target, one mechanism per contract, index scored independently of content. (4) Keep 50 ms index and 75 ms content requirements, no retrospective relaxation. (5) Ten families canonical, forty families transfer diagnostic only. (6) Frozen deployed readout primary; best-over-grid only an explicitly optimistic diagnostic. | DECISIONS.md:778-793 |

**Seeds.**

- exploration: Seed 44 (correlated F=10/s=60): 'existing exploratory seed 44' (contract:19); P2-E5 exploration seeds were 44-45 (docs/plant2/P2-E5-structured-items.md:16). Seed 90 (duty): 'existing diagnostic seed 90' (contract:21); P2-E4 seed 90 was the reviewer's probe on the frozen driver, with pre-contract exploratory twin-A M=1000 main/duty R_3 store 0.995/0.995, online-written store 0.83/0.205, size-matched random 0.885/0.71, labelled 'Exploratory evidence (not results)' (docs/plant2/EVAL-after-P2-E4.md:120-127). Both seeds are REUSED and 'already informed the hypotheses and numerical expectations' (review/plant2/P2-R1/findings-results-methodology.md:47). RESULTS-P2-R1.md:4: 'two predeclared exploratory seeds, not a new gated PASS'.
- calibration: None. 'Readout frozen at J_fb=2.80 and g=0.3 ... No grid or calibration.' (contract:33); RESULTS-P2-R1.md:48 'No threshold grid was used to improve these numbers.' R_3 threshold k=3 fixed, not tuned (contract:55; review M5).
- gated: None. P2-R1 creates no PASS label and spends no gated seed. (Baseline gated seeds audited as committed aggregates only, not re-run: P2-E4 seeds 16-20, P2-E5 seeds 21-25, bench/results/p2r1-audit.jsonl:1 / analysis/p2r1_baseline_audit.py:96.)
- spent or reserved: Spent (reused, completed both loads): correlated seed 44 and duty seed 90. Not spent: optional replications correlated seed 45 and duty seed 91 ('If resources permit', contract:26-27; 'optional seeds 45 and 91 were not spent', RESULTS-P2-R1.md:5). No new seed is reserved by P2-R1; later takeover experiments use separate seeds (P2-E6 development 101, held-out 102, gated 111-115 per docs/plant2/HANDOFF-takeover-20261012.md:116-117 - outside P2-R1 scope).
- notes: The 200 cues within one seeded network share stores and training history; they are not 200 independent networks (review M3, findings-results-methodology.md:47). 'No optional replication or fresh gated seed was run.' Run IDs: correlated-s44-primary, duty-s90-primary (bench/results/p2r1.jsonl:1,3).

PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS=1 python3 -u -m analysis.p2r1_replay --regime correlated --seed 44 --run-id YOUR_NEW_RUN_ID (docs/plant2/RESULTS-P2-R1.md:61)
PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS=1 python3 -u -m analysis.p2r1_replay --regime duty --seed 90 --run-id YOUR_OTHER_NEW_RUN_ID (docs/plant2/RESULTS-P2-R1.md:62)

python3 -m analysis.p2r1_replay --regime {correlated|duty} --seed N [--loads 500 1000] [--results PATH] [--run-id ID]  (analysis/p2r1_replay.py:352-362; loads restricted to choices 500, 1000)

Caveats: Use fresh run IDs: raw and compact artifacts are created exclusively ('xb') and attempt.json reserves the run id, so a completed run is never overwritten or auto-cleaned (analysis/p2r1_replay.py:295-349; contract:38-39). A rerun is a REPRODUCTION with new run IDs and, if the runtime differs, 'record runtime versions and any disagreement instead of requiring cross-platform bit identity' (contract:14-15); it is not a new accepted attempt and does not change this record. Original runtime Python 3.12.14, NumPy 2.3.5. Runtime about 123 s (correlated) and 247 s (duty); the code reviewer cautions the tiny-test time should not be extrapolated and full-scale runtime was not measured by them. Per-load wall_s in the jsonl are cumulative. Portable evidence covers required criterion aggregates; out_of_window_spikes is raw-replay-only. Store/raster digests and simulation-preservation flags were not independently regenerated by the results reviewer; the check flags are constants emitted after raise-guards pass. Writer precision/recall values in this extraction are rounded to 4 d.p. from jsonl doubles unless the source quotes them. This extraction was read-only: no simulation or test was run.

**Conclusions.**

- P2-R1 completed its two mandatory reported diagnostics (correlated seed 44, duty seed 90, at M=500 and 1000); optional seeds 45 and 91 not run. (docs/plant2/RESULTS-P2-R1.md:4-5; review/plant2/P2-R1/findings-results-methodology.md:3)
- All predeclared quantitative predictions held on the specified mandatory runs (corr500, corr1000, duty1000), on reused exploratory seeds whose values partly informed the predictions. (RESULTS:23; review:28-31,47)
- Correlated M500: burst-selected R3 raised settled content completion from 9.0% to 96.5%, versus 30.0% for count-matched random writers; but oldest index specificity 84/100 still fails. (review:65; RESULTS:13-22)
- Duty M1000: online-written R3 store scored 99.5% on a settled replay versus 20.5% inherited R1, 71.0% random, 65.5% anti-selected; writer identity matters beyond writer count on the tested rasters. (review:65; RESULTS:27-28)
- Correlated M1000 remains poor: R3 content 16.0%, index specificity (C2) 50.0%, oldest index specificity 40.0%. Cleaning the tested feedback store cannot close this case; index interference is unchanged by every feedback swap. (RESULTS:34-35; review:65)
- R3 recovers the externally assigned plateau key from activity (micro precision/recall .9975/.9657 correlated 1000; .9986/.9997 duty 1000), which is evidence for recovering an assigned key, not selecting an autonomous representation. (RESULTS:25-30)
- In duty/1000 a direct matched-raster intervention supports a large feedback-contamination effect (.205 to .995); part of the gain can come from sparsity (random control .710), the remainder from which observed responders write. (RESULTS:36-38)
- Settled-copy R3 results do not demonstrate live access, online R3 coordination or O4/O5 repair; live duty/1000 inherited content joint is .545 and memory-recall-plus-content .510. (RESULTS:39-41; review M1)
- Habituation/recovery and capacity were not newly measured; two fixed-size load checkpoints are not capacity growth, a saturation law or graceful forgetting; no additive decomposition of failure is established. (RESULTS:42-46)
- Baseline audit: the frozen P2-E4/E5 FAIL verdicts and counts are supported by independent aggregate recomputation (not raw-data replication); material interpretive errors were found and appended as errata (pooled control not frequency-matched; settled readout called online; O4/O5 not feedback-invariant; unpersisted replay inputs; prototype size/load; overbroad impossibility claims). No historical gate or verdict relabelled. (DECISIONS.md:813-820; STAGES.md:104-109)
- No change to E1-E5 verdicts, Stage 1 status (incomplete) or deployed capability follows; no Stage 2 work. Forward-index interference remains the next primary mechanism target; the next primary experiment selected was P2-E6 (contract 6733109), without deploying R3, retuning readout, inhibition or recurrence. (RESULTS:49,71-72; DECISIONS.md:836-838; STAGES.md:116-124)
- Suggested precise results summary (reviewer): see findings-results-methodology.md:65. All quantitative predictions held, but reused seeds and settled swaps establish neither live online R3 recall nor autonomous key selection and do not close Stage 1.

**Open questions.**

- Does R3 (or any burst-selected writer) work live online with simultaneous fast access and clean content? 'No live R3 result' (DECISIONS.md:835); no new online R3 measurement was required to close the diagnostic.
- Does the R3 benefit replicate on the unspent optional seeds (correlated 45, duty 91) or on fresh gated seeds? STAGES.md:123-124: 'First establish evidence durability, then fresh explanatory replication.'
- Which forward-index mechanism (error-gated vs load-gated forward eligibility) repairs the correlated 500/1000 index failure (C2 .500, oldest specificity .84/.40)? Evidence 'does not discriminate'; owner ruling targets forward-index interference next.
- Does failure persist after an exact-degree item-line shuffle (reviewer-proposed distinct diagnostic third pass)? Later P2-R2 lists a formal two-seed result INVALID (STAGES.md:118).
- How do habituation/recovery (O4/O5) rescore if feedback changes, given the composite 'both' dependence (1,009/50,000 presentations)?
- Capacity growth, saturation law and graceful forgetting remain untested (two fixed-size load checkpoints only).
- Can an activity-driven representation mechanism remove the supplied assigned key? STAGES.md:124: 'a later activity-driven representation mechanism requires a separately frozen hypothesis and controls.'
- Does the out-of-window spike count matter substantively? If so, preserve portable evidence or flag as raw-replay-only (review M6).

**Experimenter supply remaining.**

- Assigned random plateau assemblies / key (the key A that R3 recovers; 'Supplied plateau keys remain', STAGES.md:116)
- Marked write timing and eligibility timing (externally scheduled BTSP writes the random plateau key before the burst is measured)
- Sensory eligibility (all writers store the same eligible sensory lines with the same clipped union rule)
- Near-verbatim sensory values / one-to-one verbatim values stored in the feedback layer
- Discrete encoding boundaries and input statistics (F=10/s=60 generator, independent random sparse items for duty)
- Rest/settle/test protocols (settled copied network; 50 s settle in the P2-E3 protocol), schedules (I=2000 ms duty schedule) and scoring labels
- Fixed readout and thresholds: J_fb=2.80, g=0.3, 50 ms index / 75 ms content windows, R3 threshold k=3 (frozen, not tuned)
- Write oracle and random key inherited from P2-E4 (P2-E4 'removes nothing at learning time', docs/plant2/P2-E4-online-memory.md:769)
- Writer selection rules for controls (random and anti-selected subsets of exact size |R3| from observed responders) and evaluation-only access to A for precision/recall
- Per the handoff: threshold accommodation uses activity but 'alone is not independent cognition'; Stage 1 remains incomplete (docs/plant2/HANDOFF-takeover-20261012.md:109-112)

**Capabilities and hypotheses.** Capabilities claimed by this record are listed in RESEARCH-MAP.md; hypotheses in HYPOTHESES.md.

**Objective.** Test whether redistributing a fixed expected encoding (binary-toggle flip) budget toward less frequently eligible sensory lines improves index specificity under partial cues without silencing the index. Hypothesis text: "Frequent sensory lines acquire synapses into many assigned assemblies and therefore recruit siblings under partial cues. Redistributing a fixed encoding budget toward less frequent eligible inputs should improve specificity without obtaining it by silencing the index." (docs/plant2/P2-E6-load-gated-encoding.md lines 9-13). Presynaptic load chosen over reconstruction error because P2-E5 inherited feedback is "already badly contaminated" (lines 15-19). Labelled "a computational metaplasticity proxy, not a biological implementation claim" and "our testable engineering hypothesis. No claim that this is a novel scientific invention" (lines 21-26).

| what | kind | detail | source |

| F10/1000 C2 specificity 179/200 below the 0.90 bar (180/200) | failed_hypothesis | Candidate C2 .895 (baseline .500, random .460). 'These are failures regardless of their proximity to the bars. Mechanism 'rejected under its frozen acceptance criteria'; direction and size of the paired gain nonetheless large (+.395 vs baseline, +.435 vs random). Failure is one passing cue short, 'not rounding artifacts'. | docs/plant2/RESULTS-P2-E6.md lines 21-24; review/plant2/P2-E6/findings-results.md lines 28, 65 |
| F10/1000 oldest-100 specificity 89/100 below the 0.90 bar (90/100) | failed_hypothesis | Candidate .890 (baseline .470, random .420). Predicted by contract as 'the oldest-item bar as the most likely failure'. The global specificity failure is not confined to the oldest 100 (11 failures oldest, 10 elsewhere); age strata .8667/.9070/.8944 show no monotonic gradient; age alone not shown to cause remaining errors. | docs/plant2/RESULTS-P2-E6.md lines 21, 62-63; review/plant2/P2-E6/findings-results.md lines 28, 49 |
| Canonical content at M1000 remains poor despite index gain | unresolved_result | Candidate inherited content joint/oldest D4 .065/.020 at 1000 (.785/.610 at 500); assigned-A diagnostic .425/.390 at 1000. 'An index improvement has therefore not solved content reconstruction.' Contract predicted 'content can still fail despite index gains'. F40 candidate content also fails (joint .200, D4 .070). No content repair deployed; cross-arm content differences reflect both changed learning responders/feedback and changed retrieval rasters, not a feedback-only effect. | docs/plant2/RESULTS-P2-E6.md lines 51-53, 132-134; review/plant2/P2-E6/findings-results.md lines 55, 57; contract line 146-147 |
| Seed-general reliability unestablished: calibration 102 and gated 111-115 unrun | unresolved_result | Result is one development seed; paired cue counts are 'paired cues within one network, not independent seed replications or statistical confidence intervals'. 'All unrun held-out/gated seeds remain unverified.' 'This supports a history-dependent assignment benefit ... on this seed. It does not establish seed-general reliability.' | docs/plant2/RESULTS-P2-E6.md lines 27-30, 94-95, 134; review/plant2/P2-E6/findings-results.md lines 3, 30 |
| Matched random control cannot isolate a causal mediator or matched operating state | inadequate_experiment | Control preserves each plateau cell's realized flips per episode but 'does not preserve which synapses change, final density or adaptive threshold state'. Candidate shows more own responses, denser store and lower threshold offsets, so 'effective operating states nevertheless differ, so this does not isolate discrimination at matched neural state'. 'A success demonstrates improved paired performance under frozen parameters, not an isolated causal mediator.' No cue-level confidence interval claimed. | docs/plant2/RESULTS-P2-E6.md lines 28-30, 41-43; review/plant2/P2-E6/findings-results.md line 47; contract lines 193-196; review/plant2/P2-E6/findings-contract-methodology.md lines 11, 25 |
| Inherited C3 novel cues cannot test rejection of related unseen exemplars | inadequate_experiment | 'Related novel exemplars are not tested by inherited C3; this limitation stays visible.' Novel C3 cues remain independent random inputs; the experiment 'cannot establish rejection of related unseen experiences'. No online recall, related-unseen-exemplar rejection, autonomous key selection or nonstationary robustness tested; regular family schedule and stationary generator limit transfer. | contract lines 174-176; review/plant2/P2-E6/findings-results.md line 63; review/plant2/P2-E6/findings-contract-methodology.md lines 23-24 |
| Code review finding P1: gated selection did not require authorized full-size attempts (seed_guard/verdict) | failed_implementation | Five full_size=False screens for 111-115 with source SMALL_INTEGRITY_TEST_ONLY produced gated PASS, reproduced entirely in memory; small-config run_seed bypassed seed guard. Closed 2026-10-12 before full-size run: selection rejects test-only screens, requires one full-size started/screen lineage, verified passing feasibility attempts, committed evidence hashes, rejects duplicates and prior gated INVALID; small runs restricted to seed 101 and non-production output paths. | review/plant2/P2-E6/findings-code.md lines 7, 37 |
| Code review finding P2: paired recall/specificity/joint evidence deferred until after optional F40 | failed_implementation | A resource stop before or during F40 would leave mandatory paired summaries empty while the screen could pass. Closed: paired discordance summaries appended immediately when each matched checkpoint is available; absent mandatory comparison keys give PARTIAL. | review/plant2/P2-E6/findings-code.md lines 9, 38 |
| Code review finding P2: unexpected implementation exceptions classified as partial resource work | failed_implementation | Broad exception handler treated ValueError/KeyError on the same path as resource exhaustion, so a screen could remain passing with an error in 'interrupted'. Commands:

```
PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 GIT_OPTIONAL_LOCKS=0 OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS=1 python3 -u -m plant2.experiments.p2_e6 run --seed 101 --attempt e6-s101-v1   (command actually used; from docs/plant2/HANDOFF-takeover-20261012.md line ~238, not a listed source; do not rerun a spent seed)
python3 -m plant2.experiments.p2_e6 {run --seed {101,102,111,112,113,114,115} [--attempt NAME] | prepare-gate | screen | verdict}   (CLI subcommands from plant2/experiments/p2_e6.py lines 796-824; 'screen' only prints stored seed_screen rows; 'verdict' appends a verdict record to bench/results/p2e6.jsonl, lines 812-815)
python3 -m pytest -q plant2/tests -m "not slow"   (AGENTS.md; 76 passed, 7 deselected per RESULTS line 85)
```

**Conclusions.**

- Seed 101 produced a complete, integrity-valid FEASIBILITY FAIL with exactly two failed parts (F10/1000 C2 179/200; F10/1000 oldest specificity 89/100); no tuning, seed 102 or gated escalation follows (docs/plant2/RESULTS-P2-E6.md lines 90-95).
- Load-aware encoding produced large paired specificity and joint-index gains at F10/1000 over both baseline and exact flip-count-matched random (C2 .500 -> .895 vs random .460; joint .440 -> .865 vs random .410), with recall .935 -> .970 (docs/plant2/RESULTS-P2-E6.md lines 17-30; DECISIONS.md lines 844-846).
- Gain is not simple silencing: own-cell responders 18.585 -> 18.925, spurious 16.970 -> 3.145, forward density .055841 -> .057623, threshold offset 5.811 -> 5.469 mV; operating states nevertheless differ (docs/plant2/RESULTS-P2-E6.md lines 39-43; review/plant2/P2-E6/findings-results.md line 47).
- The record states: 'This reduces uncertainty about the usefulness of presynaptic history in the tested regime: feature-selective encoding produces a large index gain beyond an exact flip-count control.' It does not establish autonomous key selection, abstraction, online recall, capacity growth, graceful forgetting or Stage 1 completion (docs/plant2/RESULTS-P2-E6.md lines 67-71).
- Independent-input regression passed on this seed (C1 loss .005 at 500 and .025 at 1000, within .03; content joint and D4 non-regression passed); not evidence of multi-seed robustness (docs/plant2/RESULTS-P2-E6.md lines 55-58).
- Canonical content at M1000 remains poor under deployed inherited feedback (.065 joint, .020 D4) and only .425/.390 under assigned-A diagnostic feedback: forward-index improvement is insufficient for clean content at the frozen 75 ms, J=2.8, g=.3 readout (review/plant2/P2-E6/findings-results.md line 55).
- F40 transfer: candidate meets all six absolute index bars at both loads but transfer is 'useful but mixed' (500 joint .920 vs baseline .950; recall discordance 4:10 at 1000) and content still fails; F40 cannot rescue or veto F10 (review/plant2/P2-E6/findings-results.md line 57; contract lines 188-189).
- All numerical prediction intervals held (C2 .895 in 0.70..0.95; C1 .970 in 0.85..0.98; independent C1 deltas within +/-0.05); the oldest bar failed as anticipated; accurate prediction does not soften the acceptance bars (review/plant2/P2-E6/findings-results.md line 59).
- No integrity incident, resource stop or implementation interruption occurred in the full-size run; the three code-review findings were closed before simulation (docs/plant2/RESULTS-P2-E6.md lines 98-99; review/plant2/P2-E6/findings-code.md lines 33-41).
- Disposition: failed mechanism retained as failed and preserved; DECISIONS.md 2026-10-12 selects P2-R2 (exact-degree input null, seeds 103/104) as 'the third and final takeover pass', a reported causal intervention screen, not a capability gate; 'No fourth pass or Stage2 work is authorized by this selection' (DECISIONS.md lines 841-857). STAGES.md: 'No new mechanism has passed its full acceptance screen. No Stage2 work began.' (lines 122-124).

**Limitations.**

- One development seed (101); each seed is the replication unit and no pooling or cue-level confidence interval or independent-cue p-value is claimed; paired cue fractions are 'within one network, not independent seed replications' (contract lines 113-117; docs/plant2/RESULTS-P2-E6.md lines 27-30).
- Held-out 102 and gated 111-115 unrun and unverified; no five-seed verdict (docs/plant2/RESULTS-P2-E6.md lines 94-95, 134).
- Matched random control matches attempted flips per plateau cell/episode only, not which synapses change, additions, depressions, final density or adaptive threshold state; the changed operating state may contribute; no isolated causal mediator (docs/plant2/RESULTS-P2-E6.md lines 28-30, 41-43; contract lines 193-196).
- Inherited C3 novel cues are independent random items; related unseen exemplars not tested (contract lines 174-176; review/plant2/P2-E6/findings-contract-methodology.md line 24).
- Cumulative history estimates prevalence within supplied episodes; it does not learn representations, allocation, an online write trigger or reconstructed teaching targets; regular family schedule and stationary generator limit transfer; F40 does not establish nonstationary robustness (review/plant2/P2-E6/findings-contract-methodology.md lines 23-24).
- Normalization is an aggregate over eligible dendritic inputs, 'a labelled circuit-level proxy, not a strictly pair-local rule'; preserves expected flips, not realized additions, density, firing rate or recalled signal; probabilities over 0.5 increase both potentiation and depression, old memories can suffer (contract lines 44-48).
- Metaplasticity framing is a computational proxy; Lee et al. 2010 and Wu and Maass 2025 motivate but do not validate the equation; external literature not verified by the methodology reviewer (contract lines 21-26; review/plant2/P2-E6/findings-contract-methodology.md line 3).
- Full neural dynamics and reconstruction-layer dynamics were not rerun by the independent results reviewer; encoding spike/threshold-state equality inspected not regenerated (docs/plant2/RESULTS-P2-E6.md lines 126-128; review/plant2/P2-E6/findings-results.md line 13).
- Content (inherited feedback) at M1000 remains poor in canonical F10 and F40; cross-arm content differences jointly reflect changed learning responders/feedback and retrieval rasters (review/plant2/P2-E6/findings-results.md line 55).
- learning.probability_min/max fields describe candidate probabilities in all arms; baseline deploys p=.5 (review/plant2/P2-E6/findings-results.md line 61).
- A pseudocount of one is not mathematically identical to the original 0.8-equivalent prior (review/plant2/P2-E6/findings-contract-methodology.md line 26).
- Selection through the two-seed feasibility screen must be disclosed beside any later five-seed result (review/plant2/P2-E6/findings-contract-methodology.md line 27).
- Raw cache for e6-s101-v1 not present on this machine at extraction time (see missing_sources).

**Open questions.**

- Does the F10/1000 specificity gain replicate on held-out seed 102 and fresh gated seeds 111-115, and would the candidate pass the bars there? (unrun; docs/plant2/RESULTS-P2-E6.md lines 94-95, 134)
- Is the gain due to better discrimination or the changed operating point (own responders, density, threshold offsets differ)? Not isolated at matched neural state (review/plant2/P2-E6/findings-results.md line 47).
- Can clean content at M1000 be achieved given the forward-index gain? Assigned-A only reaches .425/.390; no content repair deployed (docs/plant2/RESULTS-P2-E6.md lines 52-53, 132-134).
- Does a distinct input-structure explanation (higher-order co-occurrence vs exact per-line input degree) account for the effect? To be tested diagnostically by the exact-degree input null (P2-R2), 'not a revision or rescue of E6'; it 'cannot guarantee removal of every dependency or exact matching of realized spike eligibility' (review/plant2/P2-E6/findings-results.md line 63; DECISIONS.md lines 851-857).
- Does the mechanism reject related unseen exemplars (not tested by inherited C3)? (contract lines 174-176)
- Online recall, capacity growth, graceful forgetting, autonomous key selection, abstraction: untested (docs/plant2/RESULTS-P2-E6.md lines 67-71).
- A later activity-driven representation mechanism requires a separately frozen hypothesis and controls (docs/plant2/STAGES.md lines 122-124).
- STAGES next step after the takeover: 'First establish evidence durability, then fresh explanatory replication' (docs/plant2/STAGES.md lines 122-123).

**Experimenter supply remaining.**

- Assigned plateau keys (random plateau set supplied by the experimenter; 'assigned plateau probability' inherited) - 'No assigned-key ... supply is removed' (contract lines 153-154; docs/plant2/RESULTS-P2-E6.md lines 70-71).
- Write timing / encoding boundaries (episode boundaries, 200+50 ms quiet-learning schedule) (contract lines 49-53, 153-154; docs/plant2/RESULTS-P2-E6.md line 71; review/plant2/P2-E6/findings-contract-methodology.md line 5).
- Verbatim sensory values and input design (family/exemplar generator, near-verbatim values) (docs/plant2/RESULTS-P2-E6.md lines 70-71).
- Test-protocol supply: 50 s settle and test scheduling, half/novel/full cue sets, evaluation on frozen copies (contract lines 153-154, 170-176; docs/plant2/RESULTS-P2-E6.md line 71).
- A sparsity prior and global (aggregate) normalization over eligible inputs with fixed budget 0.5 * |E_k| and pseudocount 1 (review/plant2/P2-E6/findings-contract-methodology.md line 5; contract lines 36-47).
- Inherited responder-written feedback store and, as diagnostic only, the assigned-A feedback store (supplied key); neither assigned-A feedback nor R3 is deployed by this mechanism (docs/plant2/RESULTS-P2-E6.md lines 52-53; contract lines 78-80).
- Frozen readout and neural parameters (J_mem=1.525, accommodation tau=10000, J_fb=2.80, g=0.3, 50 ms index / 75 ms content windows) and frozen bars and cue sets (contract lines 49-54).
- Contract states what success would and would not change: 'A success would reduce dependence on uniformly independent input statistics, not demonstrate autonomous representation, abstraction or reasoning' (contract lines 153-155). The mechanism removes no assigned-key, write-timing, verbatim-value or test-protocol supply (contract lines 152-154).

**Capabilities and hypotheses.** This record demonstrates no capability in the README's sense. It has one development seed (101), its frozen feasibility screen returned FEASIBILITY FAIL, and calibration seed 102 and gated seeds 111-115 were never run. Its single-seed contrasts are `observed` at most, so none of them enters the capability table in RESEARCH-MAP.md. The hypotheses this record tests are in HYPOTHESES.md. Any hypothesis tested only on this development seed is `under_test` or `mixed`, never `supported`.

**Objective.** 'Does substantial F10/s60 index interference remain when input-line usage is held exactly fixed but the original grouping of lines into related episodes is randomized?' (docs/plant2/P2-R2-degree-null.md L25-27). Estimand: 'performance under a finite, block-constrained randomized input intervention versus the original input sequence'; explicitly not 'an independent causal partition into a frequency contribution and a correlation contribution' (contract L35-41).

**Seeds.**

- **exploration:** None for R2. No exploration, tuning or calibration seed is declared; contract states 'There is no substitute easier family count and no tuning on the resulting scores' (contract L32-33). Seeds 103 and 104 are the only simulation seeds; both are fresh, mandatory, and used identically (ORIGINAL and DEGREE_NULL arms, loads 500 and 1000).
- **calibration:** No full-size calibration. The only control is the small original/sham identity check on synthetic seed 103 (m=n=256, a=64, f_q=0.05, 20 episodes): 'an implementation check, not full-size calibration' (contract L150-151). 'No second full-size sham simulation' and 'Do not run a fresh full-size benchmark'; runtime was estimated from E6 inherited-baseline records (about83 s and about72 s) (contract L11-14, L261).
- **gated:** None. 'Two seeds are explanatory screening, not a five-seed capability gate' (contract L199-200); 'It is a reported causal intervention screen, not a capability gate' (DECISIONS.md L855-856, selection entry headed L841). Each paired artifact's scope: 'two-seed descriptive contrast, no causal partition or capability gate' (bench/results/p2r2/seed103-M1000-paired.json). For context only (other experiment): E6 held-out seed 102 and gated seeds 111-115 remain untouched (HANDOFF L116-118, L151-152).
- **spent or reserved:** 103 and 104 are SPENT: 'R2 seeds103/104 are now spent for development informed by this evidence' (HANDOFF L246-247); production drivers keep permanent seed/resource reservations, 'do not reset them to obtain another result' (HANDOFF L244-245). Seed 104 stays INVALID with no replacement or rerun. Other seeds mentioned and unspent: R1 optional 45/91 not run (HANDOFF L92-93, L246); E6 held-out 102 and gated 111-115 untouched.
- **notes:** Seed audit at freeze: no recognized seed fields for 103/104 in repository result journals or source/contract/cache inventory; incidental occurrences as cue counts or line numbers are not seed use (contract L20-21, L132-135). Stream keys: items (seed,21,F,k); null chains (seed,40,F,block,chain). Poisson uniform variates stay aligned tick by tick across arms; actual sensory spikes and BTSP coin draws do not (contract L153-159).

**Reproducibility.**

Commands:

```
PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 GIT_OPTIONAL_LOCKS=0 OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS=1 python3 -u -m analysis.p2r2_degree_null run --seed 103
PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 GIT_OPTIONAL_LOCKS=0 OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS=1 python3 -u -m analysis.p2r2_degree_null run --seed 104
PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -m analysis.p2r2_degree_null summary   (read-only; reports INVALID)
PYTHONPATH="$HOME/.cache/brain-sim/takeover-deps" PYTEST_DISABLE_PLUGIN_AUTOLOAD=1 PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 GIT_OPTIONAL_LOCKS=0 BRAINSIM_CACHE="$HOME/.cache/brain-sim/YOUR_FRESH_PERSISTENT_RUN" OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS=1 python3 -m pytest --capture=sys -p no:cacheprovider -p no:tmpdir --assert=plain -q plant2/tests -m 'not slow'   (source suite: 86 passed, 7 deselected)
Driver module docstring: after committing the driver, run two workers with 'run --seed 103' and 'run --seed 104'; each executes original/null sequentially; one permanent experiment clock includes preprocessing; a started seed is never overwritten.
```

Seeds 103/104 are spent and driver reservations are permanent ('do not reset them to obtain another result'); rerunning is reproduction only, not a new accepted attempt, and no replacement under this contract is authorized. 'Do not blindly rerun spent seeds.' The recovered seed-104 original artifact reproduces recorded bytes/SHA but 'does not restore successful execution of the original paired stage'; recovery recipe: base = ORIGINAL/M1000 index portable; fill content[store] and content_summaries[store] from inherited and assigned_A portables; timing from final aggregate with learning_s omitted; json.dumps(sort_keys=True, separators=(',',':'), allow_nan=False) + newline, UTF-8; require exact byte/SHA equality before saving. (equal to the current 61-line file), whereas findings-results.md L19 says the manifest 'preserves the 60-record worker-journal snapshot; the parent's later integrity-disposition append is separate'; findings-integrity.md L9 states 61 lines with the last being the parent's INVALID disposition. Global four-process limit is not mechanically enforced ('global_max_simulations=4 ... is metadata, not a cross-namespace process detector'); lead coordinated manually. Handoff names working branch codex/plant2-takeover-20261012; local checkout branch is plant2-research-20261022.

**Conclusions.**

- Structural prediction held: fixed-degree null reduced original-label overlap contrast D to 0.00248 (seed 103) and -0.00171 (seed 104) from 14.45394 and 14.39250 at F10/1000 while preserving exact row and per-block column counts; Q fell 36.73036->18.90274 and 36.83363->19.15428; about 95.257%/95.217% of active lines replaced (RESULTS L58-59, L120).
- Valid seed 103 at 1000: C2 .470->.755, joint index .410->.675, C1 .930->.920, oldest recall .930->.920: label MATERIAL STRUCTURE SENSITIVITY met, with raw C2 bar-deficit fraction .66279; null C2 .755 and oldest specificity .680 still fail .90 bars (RESULTS L84-88). Single valid seed; explanatory screen, not a capability gate.
- 'The valid contrast reduces uncertainty: original family grouping is not necessary for high-load failure, and the fixed-degree input intervention materially changes performance. It does not show that line frequency is the sole residual cause or that the plant learned pattern separation. No experimenter-supplied structure is removed. Stage 1 remains incomplete.' (RESULTS L133-136)
- Content failure at 1000 persists independently of index gains: inherited .005->.075 (below .10 material margin), assigned-A .130->.320; oldest content inherited .00->.07, assigned-A .11->.20 on seed 103 (RESULTS L92-101; seed attribution verified in bench/results/p2r2/seed103-*-M1000.json D4).
- Seed 103 at 500 carries a recall cost: oldest recall .980->.910 (-0.070) despite inherited content .115->.980; low-load material-gain flag false; 'do not hide that cost' (RESULTS L90-92; DECISIONS.md L866).
- Seed 104 and the formal two-seed outcome are INVALID; exact-hash recovery supports numerical recoverability only; recovered C2 .460->.715, joint .400->.640 are post hoc corroboration, not valid replication (RESULTS L8-12, L32-37).
- Cause of the persistence discrepancy remains unresolved; do not attribute to Git, concurrency or the filesystem without evidence (HANDOFF L184-185; findings-integrity L5).
- Next work per record: first validate durable, separately written, sequenced worker evidence with size/hash/completeness verification and quiescent checkpoint verification; then predeclare fresh replication of the explanatory contrast before selecting activity-driven pattern separation or a narrower load/state intervention. Do not retune E6, silently deploy R3, begin sequences, or escalate to Stage 2; no fourth pass (RESULTS L138-141; DECISIONS L860-880; capability review L23-25).
- Later owner-directed context (added from verification review omissions; outside the R2 record's L860-880 range, recorded by the verifier as uncommitted working-tree text at review time): a subsequent DECISIONS.md entry makes evidence durability (docs/plant2/EVIDENCE-DURABILITY.md, plant2/evidence.py) the first engineering change and states 'no new seed is spent before it exists. Frozen drivers are not retrofitted.' (DECISIONS.md L883-905).
- Explicitly not established: 'No autonomous keys, online capability, related-unseen-example rejection, new learning mechanism, Stage1 completion or Stage2 authorization was established' (findings-results L76); the takeover shows 'partial selectivity effects and sharper diagnostics, not accepted robust memory, autonomous keys, capacity growth, abstraction or independent cognition' (DECISIONS L878-880); capacity growth, graceful forgetting, robust online access and autonomous representation remain unestablished (capability review L11).
- Takeover-level context from the capability review (other experiments, not R2): R1 duty/1000 R3 content .995 vs inherited .205 vs count-matched random .710 on a settled copy; E6 seed101 canonical1000 joint index .440->.865 but fails frozen specificity screen (C2=179/200, oldest 89/100 per contract L5); inherited content .065 and assigned-A .425; R1 and E6 gains 'cannot be added into an unmeasured combined system' (takeover-capability-review.md L5-9).

**Limitations.**

- Formal overall status INVALID: only seed 103 is a valid prospective measurement; seed 104 values (including the 1000-item paired comparison, reconstructed after the failure) are descriptive/post hoc and 'cannot establish formal replication' (RESULTS L80-82).
- Intervention changes more than line frequency: about 95% of each episode's active lines replaced; within-block cumulative-count deviation max 7/9, RMS 0.86565/0.86611; actual eligibility column counts differ up to 3; BTSP coin draws desynchronize (713/722 episodes, final +338/-386); threshold offsets and responder counts change. Paired common-seed sampling, not spike- or coin-matched; no additive frequency-vs-correlation decomposition (RESULTS L103-116).
- Curveball budget (10,000 trades per block) is a fixed engineering budget, not a proven mixing-time bound; passing heuristic checks does not establish uniform sampling; only the fixed chain-0 endpoint was used (contract L78-81).
- Block constraints preserve coarse learning-age marginals (including the oldest 100 as a group) but not individual-line interarrival times, within-block cumulative counts, higher-order co-occurrence, or which lines coincide with a plateau (contract L122-125).
- Assigned-A feedback is a supplied reference store, 'not a deployed mechanism or guaranteed upper bound'; content contrasts combine changed learned feedback with changed own retrieval rasters (contract L186-187; findings-results L76).
- Two seeds are explanatory screening, not a five-seed capability gate; no pooled confidence interval or independent-cue p-values reported by design (contract L197-200).
- Absolute bars still fail at 1000 on all stores: null C2 .755, oldest specificity .680, inherited content .075, assigned-A .320 (seed 103).
- Seed 103 at 500 has oldest-recall loss 0.070 (.980->.910) despite better specificity and content.
- F40 evidence is structural only; no F40 neural trajectory was authorized or run.
- Persistence integrity: no artifact/journal fsync, no journal lock, no per-worker sequence chain, no post-append readback; paired_evidence does not verify size/hash before parsing; stage receipts attest append calls in memory, not later durability; cause of loss unknown (findings-integrity L27-31).
- Global four-process concurrency and E6-complete precondition were enforced by the lead manually, not mechanically ('global_max_simulations=4' is metadata) (findings-code L29).
- Independent reviewers did not rerun neural dynamics, Curveball chains, or threshold trajectories: chain attempted/changed counters checked for consistency, not replayed; unchanged-learning-state and threshold capture source-inspected only (findings-results L25, L29); code reviewer did not measure full-size runtime or RSS.
- Required safety pytest wrapper cannot collect (genuine 'reflex' package absent); only a qualified standalone scan (12 grandfathered findings, no new) was run (HANDOFF L49-55).
- Source disagreement on journal snapshot size: seed104-recovery-provenance.json records journal_snapshot records=61 (SHA256 305e3edd..., equal to current 61-line file); findings-results.md L19 describes a 60-record worker-journal snapshot plus a separate parent append; findings-integrity.md L9 reports 61 lines with the last the parent's INVALID disposition.
- Contract-review provenance gap: the DRAFT as committed in 2454f06 already contains the corrected structural-stop chain scope ('on each chain's endpoint', 'separately within each chain', 'between the two chain endpoints') and label-3 'original C2 <0.90' wording, identical to the final contract (DRAFT L99-103, L199; contract L112-116, L210). The pre-review draft text that findings-contract.md reviewed is therefore not in git history. The ledger records all three contract findings as addressed (review/ledger.jsonl L465-467), and the driver applies all structural checks to both chains (structural_decision L221-235).
- The record explicitly denies autonomous keys, online capability, related-unseen-example rejection, new learning mechanism, capacity growth, graceful forgetting, abstraction, Stage1 completion and Stage2 authorization (findings-results L76; DECISIONS L878-880; capability review L11).
- Latency/first-spike numeric summaries and per-stage wall-time breakdowns are not stated in the listed prose sources; latency medians exist only in the portable artifacts.
- Seed attribution still inferred, not verified: eligibility RMS .24285/.24382, coin-draw 713/722 and maxima 950/1010, novel rejection .99/.99 vs 1.00/1.00 (original/null order); only +338 is explicitly attributed to seed 103 (RESULTS L114-115).

|---|---|---|---|
| contract_methodology (independent preimplementation, reviewed the DRAFT) | F1: make activation match the authorization (require observed FEASIBILITY FAIL under E6's frozen seed101 screen, completed mandatory evidence, terminated E6 simulations, explicit lead selection and freeze); E6 PASS, INVALID or PARTIAL must not satisfy it; 'main material discrepancy' | accepted; final contract header records E6 FEASIBILITY FAIL (C2=179/200; oldest specificity=89/100), all 16 E6 loads complete, no active E6 process (ledger L465) | review/plant2/P2-R2/findings-contract.md L7; contract L3-6 |
