**Open questions.**

- What caused the zero-byte seed104-ORIGINAL-M1000.json and the two missing journal events (ORIGINAL/M1000 load_complete, DEGREE_NULL/M500 load_started)? Unresolved, accepted_open.
- Does the material structure sensitivity replicate on two valid, freshly predeclared seeds under durable, separately written, sequenced evidence with size/hash/completeness checks?
- How much of the benefit is due to line frequency vs family co-occurrence vs fine timing, cue composition, actual eligibility, coin alignment and adaptive state? Not identified by this design.
- Does the F10 effect transfer to F40 neurally? No F40 trajectory was authorized.
- Why does seed 103 lose oldest recall .980->.910 at M=500 while specificity and content improve?
- Would an activity-driven pattern-separation mechanism before storage learn a less-confusable representation from original F10 inputs while preserving old memories, or is a narrower load/operating-state experiment the better next step? Selection condition not yet met.
- Is the 10,000-trade chain adequate for independent-draw behavior? Not certified.
- Is assigned-A feedback an upper bound? Record says no; it may itself fail (.320/.325 at 1000).
- How do changed learned feedback and changed retrieval rasters separately contribute to the content contrasts?
- Which durability design (sequenced per-worker journals, exclusive immutable receipts, explicit fsync, descriptor verification before evidence consumption) will be adopted? Requires a separately reviewed source change.

**Experimenter supply remaining.**

- Sparse sensory coordinates and values (near-verbatim sensory values): F10/s60 FamilyGen inputs, 100 lines per episode in a 4000-line population, and the null's block structure.
- Discrete encoding episodes and encoding boundaries (200 ms input per episode, inherited E3).
- Random plateau assemblies supplied as memory keys ('Assigned plateau sets remain the supplied random memory keys'); A matched across arms.
- Eligibility timing and quiet learning protocol.
- Prescribed partial cues: positional half masks, independent novel half cues, full cues (200 each) with 100 ms cue and 200 ms background.
- Settle/test scheduling and rest protocol (50 s settle, checkpoint deep copies at 500 and 1000).
- Input statistics and the randomization recipe itself: row Curveball trade, 10,000 trades, 100-episode blocks, chain stream keys, fixed seeds 103/104.
- Frozen readout windows (50 ms index, 75 ms content) and scoring labels/bars (inherited .90 bars etc.).
- Assigned-A written feedback store as a supplied diagnostic reference.
- Original family labels as diagnostic annotations (never used by generator or learner).
- 'The plant has not learned when to store, how to allocate its own keys, or when to retrieve.' 'No experimenter-supplied structure is removed.'

**Capabilities and hypotheses.** Capabilities claimed by this record are listed in RESEARCH-MAP.md; hypotheses in HYPOTHESES.md.

**Objective.** Repeat P2-R2's contrast ("Does substantial F10/s60 index interference remain when input-line usage is held exactly fixed but the original grouping of lines into related episodes is randomized?", `docs/plant2/P2-R2-degree-null.md:25-27`) on two fresh seeds with durable, verified evidence, because P2-R2's formal two-seed outcome is INVALID (seed 104's combined artifact was found empty at the paired read) and every reviewer of the takeover asked for durable evidence followed by fresh replication before a mechanism is chosen on this contrast (`P2-R2b-replication.md:7-14`). Hypotheses tested: H-044 (alias H-R2, material structure sensitivity at M = 1,000) and its riders H-045 (a low-load oldest-recall cost) and H-046 (inherited content below the bar at 1,000); P3 also bears on H-043. The estimand is unchanged from P2-R2: performance under a finite block-constrained randomised input intervention versus the original sequence, not a causal partition into frequency and correlation contributions.

**Reproducibility.** Run from the repository root on this PC with `BRAINSIM_CACHE=$HOME/.cache/brain-sim/plant2-research-20261022/p2r2b`, `OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS=1`, the venv at `~/.cache/brain-sim/venv` (CPython 3.11.17, NumPy 2.4.6): `python -m analysis.p2r2b_replication run --seed 105` and `--seed 106` as two concurrent workers; `python -m analysis.p2r2b_replication summary` for the wrapper status rule; `python -m analysis.p2r2b_replication p6` for P6. The frozen driver's source manifest requires every inherited file byte-identical to baseline 55e16eb and every listed source committed at HEAD; `analysis/p2r2_degree_null.py` executed byte-identical to c7a5350. Every artifact under `bench/results/p2r2b/` carries a recorded size and SHA-256 in `seed_complete.result.completed_stages` and in its worker journal; `plant2.evidence.verify_artifact` re-checks any of them, and `verify_journal` re-checks the chain. The pre-run gate (13 `test_p2_r2b.py` tests with the non-slow suite, 116 passing; safety lint showing only the 12 grandfathered findings) was run before the workers started. "No git command ran during the window" is the operator's account, not a recorded field (erratum 6). The CPython/NumPy version difference from P2-R2 is confounded with seed for any disagreement with P2-R2 (`P2-R2b-replication.md:100-103`).

**Conclusions.** (Wording per the errata.) With P2-R2's valid seed 103, three valid seeds now show that randomising which lines co-occur, while holding every line's count within each 100-episode block exact, removes about 60 % of the measured C2 bar deficit at M = 1,000 and leaves high-load specificity and content below their bars. The original family grouping is therefore not necessary for high-load failure. The C2 deficit at M = 1,000 is materially sensitive to co-occurrence structure under block-constrained randomisation; this design does not apportion the failure between line load and co-occurrence (erratum 1). The low-load oldest-recall cost seen on seed 103 is not a reliable feature of the contrast (P5). The condition the independent capability review set for selecting a co-occurrence-sensitive mechanism ("replicated material structure sensitivity without the declared recall loss", `review/plant2/takeover-capability-review.md`) is met on two fresh seeds; this informs, and does not by itself authorise, the next mechanism contract (erratum 5). Nothing here is a memory capability (`RESULTS-P2-R2b.md:72-82`). The evidence module behaved as contracted: every artifact was created exclusively, fsynced, read back and hashed at the write; the two combined load artifacts were re-verified immediately before each `paired_evidence` read and every artifact after `run()` returned (erratum 2); P2-R2's empty-artifact failure mode would have been caught at the first verification.

**Limitations.**
- A finite block-constrained randomisation is not a causal partition into frequency and correlation contributions; fine timing, actual eligibility, coin alignment and adaptive state change with the inputs (`P2-R2b-replication.md:160-162`).
- Two seeds are explanatory screening, not a five-seed capability gate; the replication unit is the seed and no pooled interval is computed (inherited from `P2-R2-degree-null.md:197-200`).
- Paired, not identical, sampling: coin draw counts differ between arms and eligibility differs by 2-3 episodes per block (`RESULTS-P2-R2b.md:48-50`).
- Runtime differs from P2-R2 (CPython 3.11.17 / NumPy 2.4.6 against 3.12.14 / 2.3.5) and is confounded with seed (`P2-R2b-replication.md:100-103`).
- P6's magnitude was set from one prior seed; its failure on seed 106 says nothing about the direction of the effect, which was the same on both seeds (descriptive).
- The cause of P2-R2's seed-104 empty artifact is not diagnosed by this run; removing git operations during the window and seeing no recurrence on two seeds is not a diagnosis.
- The result informs the mechanism choice but authorises nothing on its own; the choice goes through the roadmap checkpoint (`RESULTS-P2-R2b.md:112-114`).

**Review findings acted on.**

| reviewer | finding | disposition | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| architecture-reviewer (draft contract, pre-freeze) | eight findings, among them: the wrapper had no hook before the frozen `seed_complete` (verification moved after `run()` with a wrapper-level status rule); `SOURCES` is built at import time (rebound explicitly); the cache root inserted `plant2/p2r2/` (documented, asserted); the evidence module's serialiser differed from the frozen `json_bytes` (writers made byte-equivalent, `allow_nan=False` added to `plant2/evidence.py`); predictions not decidable from named fields (P1-P6 rewritten to name record and field) | all addressed in the frozen contract 4cd865b | `P2-R2b-replication.md:4-6, 40-78, 112-136` |
| results reviewer (post-run, Opus, read-only) | 1 FIX: "line load alone does not explain the residual failure" unsupported | erratum 1 | `findings-results.md:38` |
| results reviewer | 2 FIX: "every artifact re-verified before `paired_evidence`" overstates | erratum 2 | `findings-results.md:39` |
| results reviewer | 3 FIX: seed-106 P6 explanation does not fit the data | erratum 3 | `findings-results.md:40` |
| results reviewer | 4 NOTE: assigned-A at 1,000 is MATERIAL GAIN | erratum 4 | `findings-results.md:41` |
| results reviewer | 5 NOTE: "condition now met" overreaches and lacks a citation | erratum 5 | `findings-results.md:42` |
| results reviewer | 6 NOTE: "no git ran" not backed by a record | erratum 6 | `findings-results.md:43` |
| results reviewer | 7 NOTE: P6 output not persisted | two `p6_prediction` rows appended | `findings-results.md:44`; `bench/results/p2r2b.jsonl` |

**Open questions.**
- What design would apportion the M = 1,000 failure between line load and co-occurrence? The block-constrained null cannot (erratum 1; H-043 "experiment needed").
- Why did P2-R2's seed-104 combined artifact come up empty? Still `unresolved`; P2-R2b only removed the git confound and added verification.
- Is the low-load index gain recall-preserving in general? Two fresh seeds say yes, seed 103 said no; no further seed is contracted.

**Experimenter supply remaining.** Unchanged from P2-R2: the random plateau keys, episode boundaries, eligibility timing, verbatim values and the settle/test schedule remain supplied, and the randomisation recipe (row Curveball, 10,000 trades, 100-episode blocks, chain stream keys, fixed seeds) is the experimenter's (`RESULTS-P2-R2b.md:80-82`).

**Capabilities and hypotheses.** No capability is claimed; `RESEARCH-MAP.md` lists this result under reported explanation results, not in the capability table. Hypotheses updated: H-044 (alias H-R2) `supported` on three valid seeds; H-045 `refuted`; H-046 `supported` at M = 1,000; H-043 `supported` for "grouping not necessary" with its apportioning clause removed; see `HYPOTHESES.md`.


## P2-E7: prediction-gated (error-gated) forward eligibility with a conserved flip budget (contracted 2026-10-22)

| field | value |
|---|---|
| status | complete (development seed 121 run 2026-10-22, 20:09-20:19 UTC; COMPLETE with verified evidence; independent results review pending) |
| result label | FEASIBILITY FAIL, informative (`seed_screen.screen.verdict`, `informative: true`): F10/1,000 candidate C1 .895, C2 .745, C4_spurious .720, joint index .645 against the absolute bars; every comparative margin held; no structural kill fired; integrity true (`docs/plant2/RESULTS-P2-E7.md`). Held-out seed 122 does not open. |
| stage | Stage 1, first mechanism contract under the 2026-10-22 mandate; selected at the roadmap checkpoint of that day (`docs/research/ROADMAP.md`, checkpoint 2026-10-22; `DECISIONS.md` entry of the same day) from a ten-proposal judge panel (workflow wf_848f4c6e-5b0). Owner authority: ruling 4 of 2026-10-10 (error-correcting write family) and ruling 3 of 2026-10-12 (local-error signal, forward index next, one mechanism). |
| dates | Draft v1 committed af04c89 (2026-10-22); independent adversarial read, verdict REVISE AND RE-REVIEW (fifteen findings, one restructure); draft v2 committed 64ff692; re-review, verdict FREEZE WITH THE LISTED FIXES (nine findings); frozen fede3c2 (2026-10-22, `docs/plant2/P2-E7-error-gated-eligibility.md`); frozen JSON `docs/plant2/P2-E7-frozen.json` in the following commit. |
| mechanism under test | One mechanism: during each 200 ms encoding, the memory layer's spikes in ticks 0-74 are played through the frozen readout `plant2.readout.replay` (pinned call: n_ticks 75, window 75, short 50, span 75, J [2.8], g [0.3]; the feedback store as it stands before the episode's write; rec at rest; no path back to the memory layer). The predicted set G(x) is the lines with a rec spike at tick ≤ 74; the veto is V(x) = E(x) ∩ G(x); only E'(x) = E(x) \ V(x) is toggled, with p = min(1, 0.5·\|E\|/\|E'\|) (p := 0 when E' is empty), coins drawn exactly as `btsp_update` draws them so the episode is bit-identical to E3 whenever V is empty. No new parameter. Labelled proxy: mismatch- or novelty-gated plasticity (comparator view; circuit-level proxy, not CA1 physiology). |
| contract commit | fede3c2 (full fede3c249dad7ba6237bcedcbfe498e253c3de39), `docs/plant2/P2-E7-error-gated-eligibility.md`; drafts v1/v2 kept as `P2-E7-error-gated-eligibility-DRAFT.md` (preparation provenance). |
| implementation commit | bb22d05 (full bb22d05138befeee24a275aff1756ed37c3ee7bb): `plant2/experiments/p2_e7_error_gated.py` and `analysis/p2e7_riders.py`, 73 tests in `plant2/tests/test_p2_e7.py` green with the non-slow suite (216 passed), safety lint OK; independent architecture review (ten findings, five fixed before the run, verdict RUN AFTER THE LISTED FIXES) and a small-size three-stage rehearsal preceded the run. |
| results commit(s) | the commit that adds `docs/plant2/RESULTS-P2-E7.md`, `bench/results/p2e7.jsonl` (117 rows) and `bench/results/p2e7/` (recorded by SHA in the review commit that follows); riders appended after the run (2 `rider` rows) |
| other commits | 82956d5 (ledgers), af04c89 (roadmap checkpoint, decision entry, seed registry), 64ff692 (offline-screens contract; draft v2). |

**Objective.** Does a write rule that refuses to index the parts of an experience the network already regenerates from its own memory, while holding the expected flips per plateau cell at the baseline's wherever its cap allows, remove the forward-index cross-talk on correlated items at M = 1,000 without costing recall of unrelated or old items? Hypotheses: H-060 (primary), H-055 (ruling 4's two tests: the count-matched random-drop control; the mixed-stream condition scored on its independent subset plus the oldest cohort), with H-035, H-041, H-043, H-044 and H-046 bearing.

**Configuration (summary; the contract is the specification).** Inherited from P2-E6/E3/E5 byte-identical (pins split between baseline 55e16eb for the E3-era files and the freezing commit for `p2_e6.py`, `evidence.py`, `p2r1_replay.py`): J_mem 1.525, accommodation tau 10,000 ms, J_fb 2.80, g 0.3, 50 ms index and 75 ms content windows, loads 500/1,000 as a snapshot of one run, the settled test protocol, E6's bars (C1-C4 ≥ 0.90, joint ≥ 0.85) and margins (C2 ≥ baseline + 0.20 and ≥ random + 0.10; joint ≥ each + 0.10; non-regression 0.03 index / 0.05 content) plus two declared additions (C2 ≥ history_drop + 0.05; F10/1,000 C4_recall non-regression 0.03). Conditions: F10 (eight arms: baseline, candidate, random_drop, history_drop, gate_off, no_conservation, label_oracle, assigned_A_teacher), independent (baseline, candidate), mixed (odd episodes F10 exemplars, even episodes independent items from stream (seed, 36, k); baseline, candidate; scored on the independent subset in cues: kill at ≥ 4 cues index, ≥ 5 cues content), F40 (transfer, reported only). New streams 33, 34, 35, 36. Execution in three stages: the F10 candidate learns first and is kill-checked before any test phase (no engagement, contamination-driven veto, saturated teacher, no structure, false engagement on uncorrelated input); then the other arms; then a coordinator runs the tests and cross-arm checks. Resource: 60 min wall, four processes, no git during the window. Evidence through `plant2/evidence.py` with per-worker journals.

**Seeds.** Development 121; held-out 122 only on a committed FEASIBILITY PASS on 121; gated 131-135 only on committed passing screens and a `gate_ready` record, under a separately frozen gate contract. One full-size attempt per seed; an INVALID screen locks the rest.

**Controls.** Literal E3 baseline (its forward store must equal E3 learning; `uniform_integrity`); count-matched random drop (stream 33; fails if the error signal carries no information); history-weighted drop (stream 34, E6's counts in a control only; separates a co-occurrence-sensitive prediction from a hot-line filter); gate_off integrity arm (forward SHA-256 must equal the baseline's at both checkpoints, else INVALID); no_conservation and label_oracle and assigned_A_teacher (reported/diagnostic only); independent and mixed candidates for non-regression; E6's sanity checks; H-042 matched-state diagnostics; the own-versus-spurious latency split (B-008) on every arm; riders (R_3/R_1/size-matched-random/anti-selected stores on the baseline trajectory from gate_off's persisted counts, reported only).

**Predictions.** P1-P12 as frozen in the contract (F10/1,000 candidate C2 in [0.82, 0.97], C1 ≥ 0.91, joint in [0.75, 0.93], C4_spurious in [0.70, 0.97] as the weakest; inherited content still below the bar in [0.02, 0.40]; random_drop C2 ≤ 0.55; candidate ≥ history_drop + 0.10; uncorrelated non-regression within ±0.02 and the veto on ≤ 5 % of episodes; mixed independent subset within ±2 cues; spurious50 mean ≤ 6 with own50 within ±1.0 of the baseline; integrity equalities). Most likely failure: the oldest-item specificity bar or a late veto onset.

**What it cannot show.** A pass is an index result on one development seed; content at F10/1,000 is expected to stay below its bar (H-046); the random key, episode boundaries, write oracle, verbatim value and test regime remain supplied; the rule's behaviour on exact repeats is untested and deploying it on streams with repeats needs an owner ruling (familiarity deferral); the mixed condition's correction pressure is that of F10 at M = 500.

**Capabilities and hypotheses.** None claimed. H-060 moves to `under_test` on the freezing commit's follow-up; H-055 is `under_test` through this contract's two ruling-4 tests.

**Results (seed 121, exploratory; all from `load`, `structural_check` and `seed_screen` records).** F10/1,000: baseline C1 .920 / C2 .500 / joint .425; candidate .895 / .745 / .645; random_drop .925 / .480 / .410; history_drop .945 / .635 / .595; gate_off = baseline; no_conservation .680 / .840 / .545; label_oracle .920 / .970 / .890; assigned_A_teacher .935 / .645 / .580. F10/500 candidate .950 / .970 / .920 (every bar holds). Independent: candidate identical to baseline, zero vetoes. Mixed independent subset: index counts within one cue of the baseline, content joint 34 → 48 cues. F40 (reported): candidate C2 .880 vs baseline .805 at 1,000. Structural (F10 candidate): mean |V| 12.96 / 40.37 (episodes 1-500 / 501-1,000), precision 0.817, prototype predicted 0.835, non-prototype 0.123, p capped 144 episodes, first engagement per family at episodes 44-138. Content .005 at F10/1,000 on every non-oracle arm. Riders on the baseline trajectory: R_3 content .925 at 500, .140 at 1,000 (R_1 .145 / .005). Wall 610.6 s, four workers.

**Predictions.** Held: P5, P7, P9, P10, P12. Failed: P1 (non-prototype 0.123 > 0.10), P2 (C2 .745), P3 (C1 .895), P4 (joint .645), P6 (content below the predicted range), P8 (content at 500 .280), P10b (independent-subset content +14 cues, favourable), P11 (spurious50 8.63; out-degree 618 > 511).

**Failures and errata.** failed_hypothesis (the rule as stated does not clear the bars; the comparative hypotheses supported on one development seed); the first start at 20:07:31 UTC was refused by a byte-equal contract pin before any record (fixed at 9bc6cf3, contract note 5; not an integrity incident). No validity or integrity failure; no resource stop.

**Conclusions.** The error signal is informative and co-occurrence-sensitive beyond load; corrections preserve unrelated memories; the conserved budget is load-bearing; the responder-written teacher beats the clean plateau-set teacher; the loss against the bar is late onset and coverage (oracle C2 .970 from the first episode against the candidate's .745), not precision; content stays at the floor (H-046). Not retuned. Next: independent results review, then the roadmap checkpoint.


## OFFLINE-SCREENS-20261022: four zero-seed analyses on persisted evidence (offline, not an experiment)

| field | value |
|---|---|
| status | complete (26 `offline_screen` records COMPLETE, 9 `evidence_verification` records passed) |
| result label | offline (screen A additionally proxy); descriptive; no capability, no gate, no replication |
| stage | Roadmap checkpoint 2026-10-22, priority 2; asked for by the judge panel before any contract cites the numbers |
| dates | Contract committed 64ff692 (2026-10-22); tests c8be1ce; module dadc62e; run 2026-10-22 19:38-19:39 UTC; results and D extension 5c7199b |
| mechanism under test | none; analyses of persisted P2-R2b (seeds 105/106) and P2-E6 (seed 101) evidence |
| contract commit | 64ff692, `docs/plant2/OFFLINE-SCREENS-20261022.md` |
| implementation commit | dadc62e (module, 27 tests green), 5c7199b (screen D extended to the P2-E6 files) |
| results commit(s) | 5c7199b (`bench/results/offline-screens-20261022.jsonl`, `bench/results/offline-screens-20261022/`); Results section appended to the contract in the following commit |
| other commits | — |

**Objective.** Record, before P2-E7 spends a seed: (A) whether the error gate's teacher is already saturated on the baseline trajectory (proxy, advisory); (B) the branch static count behind B-013 with its coin-parity integrity gate; (C) the own-versus-spurious first-spike latency split and a latency-cut bound (B-008's cheapest test); (D) test-time regeneration saturation of the responder-written store.

**Results (all offline).** A: on the baseline trajectory the prediction engages by block 3-4 and the teacher saturates late; over episodes 501-1,000 mean |V| 56.5-56.9 lines, prototype predicted 0.99, non-prototype predicted 0.29 (P2-E7 kill threshold 0.25), veto precision 0.72 (threshold 0.70); a warning, not a decision. B: integrity gate passed on both seeds; non-own cells per half cue at ≥ 12 cue synapses 91.9 / 86.9 point against 0.76 / 0.62 at K = 8; own cells untouched (20.3 of about 20 at every threshold). C: own first spikes median 22-24 ms, spurious median 66-69 ms with a 10th percentile of 36-41 ms; no latency cut from 20 to 50 ms clears C1 ≥ 0.90 and C2 ≥ 0.90 together; B-008 closed as a standalone mechanism. D: F10 full learned cues regenerate a median 3,638-3,643 lines at M = 1,000 (744-814 at 500; independent 133; E6 candidate 1,140; degree null 1,117-1,147); feedback density 0.313 / 0.318 at 1,000.

**Failures and errata.** None. The panel's branch number for seed 106 (0.58, drawn with a non-stream coin generator) is superseded by the stream-3 value 0.62.

**Reproducibility.** `BRAINSIM_CACHE=$HOME/.cache/brain-sim/plant2-research-20261022/offline-screens python -m analysis.offline_screens_20261022 run --screen {A,B,C,D}` then `summary`; every input verified against its descriptor before reading; single process; no simulation.

**Conclusions.** The pre-screen's warning stands beside P2-E7's structural check; the branch screen's separation stands beside the unmeasured branch firing point; B-008 does not deserve a standalone contract; the saturation counts the judges quoted are now recorded.

**Capabilities and hypotheses.** None claimed. H-060 gains offline evidence against (the warning); B-008 closed; B-013's cheapest test done.
